Abstract: The Multilevel Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithm accelerates Bayesian inversion by exploiting correlations between discretized quantities of interest across a hierarchy of model resolutions. A key practical parameter is the subsampling rate used to generate coarse-level proposals for the finer-level chains. Existing practice sets this rate equal to an integrated autocorrelation time as a proxy for proposal decorrelation, but this choice can be overly conservative and lead to substantial computational overhead. In this work we treat finite subsampling as an error-budget question. We derive a cost-error criterion for selecting a computationally efficient subsampling rate using estimates of sample variances, integrated autocorrelation times, and per-level costs. The criterion balances multilevel sampling errors across levels rather than enforcing approximate independence of the coarse proposals. We clarify the theoretical role of independence through an ideal reset-reference kernel, for which independent coarse proposals may be replaced by proposals generated from a reversible coarse-level kernel. We derive a Poisson-equation representation of the perturbation bias resulting from finite subsampling. Under a state-averaged lower-level forgetting assumption, this yields a conditional geometric perturbation-bias bound; in computation, the corresponding projected diagnostic is used as an empirical error-budget check. Numerical experiments on a highly autocorrelated elasticity beam benchmark show substantial reductions in computational cost compared with IAT-based subsampling. A second Darcy-flow inverse problem gives supporting evidence of qualitatively similar finite-subsampling behaviour in another elliptic benchmark.

</details>

Abstract: Splitting the exponential and exponential-like functions can significantly reduce the overall computational cost of exponential integrators, providing a strong motivation for their rigorous analysis. In this paper, we analyze splitting errors for two noncommuting unbounded operators and derive new representations that include the popular Lie--Trotter and Strang cases. Moreover, within the abstract framework of strongly continuous semigroups, we prove the convergence of a split version of the exponential Euler method and a second-order exponential Runge--Kutta method for semilinear problems. Numerical experiments on a nonlinear dispersive equation and a diffusion--reaction equation demonstrate the theoretical findings, stability properties, and efficiency of the proposed split exponential integrators compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [74] [FOSLS-deRhaNN: native de Rham neural classes for H(div) and H(curl) with applications to first-order system least-squares neural network methods for partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08016)
*Shun Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文构造了原生适配 H(div) 与 H(curl) 图空间的神经网络逼近类，通过 de Rham 复形固定算子映射标量/分量网络得到，无需网格或有限元模拟，并据此提出 FOSLS-deRhaNN 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络逼近难以保证输出始终落在 H(div)、H(curl) 等图空间中，且不连续界面的跳跃需精确处理；现有方法多依赖网格或有限元模拟，缺乏原生、无网格的函数空间逼近手段。

Result: 对椭圆方程，泛函等价于误差，并可处理 H^{-1} 右端项与 H^{1/2} Dirichlet 数据；以带不连续系数椭圆方程与 curl-curl 问题为实例；不连续解的线性输运与含激波的守恒律使用相同的通量类。

Conclusion: 所构造的原生逼近类能够统一处理图空间函数逼近与界面跳跃，避免了网格与有限元模拟，FOSLS-deRhaNN 在自然弱空间中具有良好的误差等价性与广泛应用前景。

Abstract: We construct neural approximation classes native to the graph spaces H(div) and H(curl), in two and three dimensions and, for H(div), in any dimension. Every realization lies in the space for all parameter values, and with kinked potentials, such as ReLU networks, the admissible jumps appear at finite width. The classes are images of scalar and componentwise networks under fixed operators of the de Rham complex, and do not involve a mesh or finite element emulation. For H(div) in R^n two native classes are given on an equal footing, with a skew-symmetric potential $A$: $\mathrm{Div}\,A+R_nq+\mathbf{h}$, with the divergence $q$ as an explicit unknown, and $\mathrm{Div}\,A+\mathbf{z}$ with an $H^1$ field $\mathbf{z}$; for H(curl) the analogous classes are $\mathrm{grad}\,φ+Sr+\mathbf{h}$ in two dimensions and $\mathrm{grad}\,φ+\mathbf{z}$ in two and three dimensions. In all of them every interface jump of the field is carried by the potential term, $\mathrm{Div}\,A$ or $\mathrm{grad}\,φ$, while the remaining part has no interface jump (it is an $H^1$ field in the regular-decomposition classes); the classes with $\mathbf{z}$ are the componentwise approach enriched by this term. Known or learned interface geometry enters the potential through factors with trainable amplitudes, and the remaining part if the divergence jumps. The classes lead to the FOSLS-deRhaNN method, first-order system least squares with de Rham neural networks, whose loss is the least-squares functional posed in the natural spaces of the weak formulation; for elliptic equations this includes $H^{-1}$ right-hand sides and $H^{1/2}$ Dirichlet data. Elliptic equations with discontinuous coefficients and curl-curl problems are treated as instances, with the functional equivalent to the error; linear transport with discontinuous solutions and conservation laws with shocks use the same flux classes.

</details>

Conclusion: 该一步IMEX Runge-Kutta框架为几何流提供了高阶、每级仅线性求解、无需预测几何与启动值的时间积分方法，并具备向平均曲率流与表面扩散等格式推广的能力。

Abstract: We propose a one-step implicit-explicit (IMEX) Runge-Kutta framework for high-order time integration of geometric flows. Starting from the Dziuk and Deckelnick-Dziuk parametric finite element formulations of curve shortening flow, we recast the spatially semidiscrete equations as systems of ordinary differential equations and construct second- and third-order schemes. Each stage requires only a linear solve. The key ingredient is a reduced-coefficient structure that allows the variational problem at each stage to be posed on the geometry generated by the preceding stage. The Runge-Kutta stages thus supply the required prediction geometry without a separate prediction procedure. The resulting schemes require no additional starting values. The framework also extends directly to the Barrett-Garcke-Nürnberg formulations of mean curvature flow and surface diffusion for curves and surfaces. Numerical experiments support the designed orders of temporal convergence and indicate that suitable coefficient choices yield mesh quality comparable to that of the corresponding first-order methods.

Abstract: Phase-field equations are fundamental examples of gradient-flow systems and require numerical solvers that preserve intrinsic energy dissipation. ### [77] [AdHImEx: Adaptively High-Order Implicit-Explicit Transport for Large Time Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08166)

Method: AdHImEx是一种Runge-Kutta格式，融合三阶显式时间步进与Crank-Nicolson隐式步进，仅在大Courant数区域激活隐式处理；空间上结合五阶有限体积离散以保持质量守恒。同时提出阶段相关的散度算子处理方法，在不同隐显RK阶段时间步及空间可变隐式性下保持常数性、消除虚假散度。

Abstract: Adaptively Implicit-Explicit (AdImEx) time stepping provides stability for large time steps for mass-conservative transport, making it attractive for the numerical representation of advection in weather and climate prediction. Existing AdImEx transport schemes become first-order accurate in the large-time-step, implicit regime and may require multiple sparse matrix solutions per time step. This work introduces AdHImEx, a new AdImEx scheme that addresses both limitations simultaneously. AdHImEx provides second-order accuracy for large Courant numbers while requiring only a single matrix solution per time step. It is a Runge-Kutta scheme that blends third-order accurate explicit time stepping and Crank-Nicolson implicit time stepping to provide numerically verified stability for Courant numbers up to 100. AdHImEx is designed for flows that are predominantly explicit, activating implicit time stepping only where large Courant numbers occur. It retains the full efficiency and third-order accuracy of the explicit time stepping in regions with small Courant numbers. In this work, it is combined with a fifth-order accurate finite-volume discretisation in space for mass conservation. A second contribution of this work is a novel stage-dependent treatment of the divergence operator that preserves constancy despite different implicit and explicit Runge-Kutta stage time steps and spatially varying implicitness, removing spurious divergence. This innovation is applicable to other AdImEx time stepping schemes. Convergence, transport, and efficiency tests demonstrate substantial improvements over previous first-order AdImEx schemes, little phase and amplitude error, accurate transport on locally refined meshes with large Courant numbers, and promising efficiency characteristics for atmospheric transport.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建非贴体切割有限元（CutFEM）框架：将异质空间几何特征嵌入并表征于规则背景网格，避免在复杂界面生成贴体网格；采用系数加权对称Nitsche格式施加界面温度与热流传递条件；引入ghost-penalty稳定化处理物理材料与背景网格间的任意小交截；提出半离散与全离散数值格式，并给出显式能量估计。

Result: 设计了包含基板、塑封料、芯片与焊球的二维和三维电子封装结构算例，验证了所提方法在求解高难度瞬态热问题时的精度、效率与可扩展性。

Conclusion: CutFEM为复杂多材料电子封装结构的瞬态热传导分析提供了一种无需界面贴体网格的有效途径，其系数加权Nitsche耦合与ghost-penalty稳定化保证了格式的稳定性与能量一致性，具备良好的精度、效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Advanced electronic packaging structures represent a key technological approach for extending Moore's Law. An electronic packaging structure consists of multiple materials with distinct properties, constituting a composite structure with complex spatial architecture. This paper develops an unfitted cut finite element method (CutFEM) for transient heat conduction simulation in multi-material electronic packaging structures with complex material interfaces. In the proposed computational framework, the heterogeneous spatial geometric features of electronic packaging structures is embedded and characterized in a regular background mesh, thereby avoiding the generation of body-fitted meshes on complicated interfaces. Interface temperature and heat-flux transmission conditions are imposed by a coefficient-weighted symmetric Nitsche formulation. In addition, a ghost-penalty stabilization controls arbitrarily small intersections between the physical materials and background meshes. Furthermore, the semi-discrete and fully-discrete numerical schemes are proposed, and an explicit energy estimate is derived in detail. Finally, two-dimensional and three-dimensional electronic packaging structures containing substrate, molding compound, die, and solder balls are designed to validate its accuracy, efficiency, and scalability in simulating challenging transient thermal problems.

</details>


### [80] [Subset selection for matrices by volume sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08576)
*Ivan Kozyrev,Alexander Osinsky*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出确定性算法FDVS，用于矩阵子集选择问题，在保持最优期望界的同时实现O(nkm)复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵子集选择问题中，体积采样在期望意义下能达到已知最紧的界，但此前具有该保证的算法复杂度对n至少是二次的，当k远小于n时性能受限，因此需要更高效的确定性算法。

Method: 提出前向去随机化体积采样（FDVS），一种确定性算法；并系统梳理体积采样方法：给出精确前向体积采样的O(nm²)算法，证明某已知确定性方法是逆向迭代体积采样的去随机化，并给出避免重复SVD降阶的改进及快速贪心前向变体。

Result: FDVS在可证明达到该最紧界的同时，渐进复杂度为O(nkm)，而此前所有具此保证的算法复杂度对n至少为二次，因此FDVS对极宽矩阵尤其有吸引力。数值实验在最优实验设计、传感器布置和DLRA-DEIM中验证并比较了各算法。

Conclusion: FDVS为矩阵子集选择提供了高效的确定性方案，并系统结构化体积采样方法体系，在宽矩阵场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: We address the Subset selection problem for matrices, where the goal is to select a subset $\mathcal{S}$ of $k$ column indices from a \enquote{short-and-fat} matrix $X \in \mathbb{R}^{m \times n}$, such that the sampled submatrix $X_{\mathcal{S}}$ has $\|X_{\mathcal{S}}^†X\|_F$ as small as possible. Our approach is centered on volume sampling, which attains the tightest known bound on this objective in expectation. As our primary contribution, we propose a new deterministic algorithm, Forward derandomized volume sampling (FDVS), which provably attains this bound and has asymptotic complexity $O(nkm)$. In contrast, the complexity of all previously known algorithms with this guarantee scales at least quadratically in $n$ when $k \ll n$, making FDVS particularly attractive for very wide matrices. In addition, we systematically structure the landscape of volume sampling methods: we propose a simple $O(nm^2)$ algorithm for exact forward volume sampling, show that a known deterministic method is in fact a derandomization of Reverse iterative volume sampling, and derive a modification of the latter that avoids repeated SVD downdating, along with a fast greedy forward variant. The algorithms are verified and compared in numerical experiments involving optimal experimental design, sensor placement, and DLRA-DEIM.

</details>


### [81] [Efficient localised model reduction for multiscale PDEs via Grassmannian interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08657)
*Christian Alber,Markus Bachmayr,Robert Scheichl,Huqing Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何插值方法，用于多尺度广义有限元法（MS-GFEM）中参数相关的最优局部近似空间计算，通过将参数对应的最优子空间视为Grassmann流形上的点并在稀疏网格上插值，实现高维参数空间的高效降维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度、参数依赖的偏微分方程因系数强异质性和高维参数空间而带来严重计算挑战。MS-GFEM中最昂贵的环节是计算参数相关的最优局部近似空间，亟需高效降维手段。

Method: 利用MS-GFEM的空间局部性，并假设局部参数依赖性，将全局问题分解为参数上低维的局部子问题；将每个参数对应的最优子空间辨识为Grassmann流形上的点，并通过稀疏网格上的Grassmann插值进行近似，从而在保持子空间几何结构的同时高效处理高维参数空间。

Result: 所得局部化模型降阶方法继承了MS-GFEM近乎指数的空间收敛率以及稀疏网格的参数收敛率；针对椭圆问题的数值实验验证了理论收敛结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的Grassmann流形几何插值方法有效解决了参数依赖最优局部近似空间的计算难题，兼具MS-GFEM的空间收敛优势和稀疏网格的参数收敛优势，为多尺度参数依赖PDE提供了高效降阶框架。

Abstract: Multiscale, parameter-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) pose severe computational challenges due to strong coefficient heterogeneity and high-dimensional parameter spaces. We develop a geometric interpolation approach within the multiscale generalized finite element method (MS-GFEM) that targets the most expensive component: computing parameter-dependent optimal local approximation spaces. Leveraging the spatial localization of MS-GFEM and assuming local parameter dependence, we decompose the global problem into parametrically low-dimensional local subproblems. The optimal subspaces for each parameter are identified as points on a Grassmann manifold and approximated via Grassmann interpolation on sparse grids, which preserves the geometric structure of these spaces while efficiently handling high-dimensional parameter spaces. The resulting localized model reduction method inherits the nearly exponential spatial convergence of MS-GFEM and the parametric convergence rates of sparse grids. Numerical experiments for elliptic problems confirm the theoretical convergence results.

</details>


### [82] [Effective computation of Moore-Penrose inverses over fields of rational functions by specializations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08676)
*Juana Sendra*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文研究了Moore-Penrose域上多参数有理函数矩阵的伪逆，证明Penrose条件可用线性代数求解，给出伪逆分子分母次数界（仅依赖元素次数与秩），并精确刻画了特殊化后仍为伪逆的参数值（即最大子式所构成多项式的零点、秩下降点），从充分条件提升为充要条件，提供符号算法并在Maple上测试及经济模型应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典伪逆计算涉及二次多项式系统，且以往关于特殊化后仍返回伪逆的参数值仅有充分条件，缺乏精确刻画，同时伪逆复杂度界往往依赖矩阵维度而非秩。

Method: 在Moore-Penrose域上，仅用线性代数求解Penrose条件；对多参数有理函数矩阵，用最大子式构造多项式，从矩阵本身出发（在计算伪逆之前）判定特殊化失效的参数值；给出符号算法。

Result: 伪逆分子与分母的次数界由元素次数和秩决定（不依赖矩阵维度）；失效参数值恰为最大子式所成多项式的零点，也即秩下降点，从而将先前充分条件变为充要刻画；在Maple中实现并对972次计时复制测试，应用于Leontief经济模型，失效点对应经济不再可行的临界值。

Conclusion: 该工作将特殊化问题从充分条件提升为精确刻画，给出秩与元素次数控制的伪逆次数界，并提供可实现的符号算法，兼具理论完备性与实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper we consider matrices whose entries are rational functions of several parameters over a Moore-Penrose field, that is, a field with an involutory automorphism over which every matrix has Moore-Penrose inverse. We prove that over any such field the Penrose conditions can be solved with linear algebra alone, even though they form a polynomial system of degree two. We then bound the degrees of the numerator and of the denominator of the pseudoinverse in terms of the degree of the entries and of the rank, and not of the dimensions of the matrix. Specialization is the thread: the closed form is computed once over the field of rational functions, and the question is at which parameter values it still returns the pseudoinverse of the specialized matrix. We determine the values where it does not, from the matrix alone and before computing the pseudoinverse. They are the zeros of a polynomial built from the maximal minors, and they are the values at which the rank decreases. This turns previous sufficient conditions into an exact characterization. The theory also gives symbolic algorithms for the real, the complex and the parametric case, which we implement in Maple and test on 972 timed replications. We also apply them to a Leontief economic model, where the excluded value is the point at which the economy stops being viable.

</details>


### [83] [Large Growth Happens: Gaussian Elimination with Partial Pivoting on Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08701)
*Daniel A. Spielman,Xifan Yu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 证明了带部分主元的高斯消元法在 n×n 随机矩阵上的增长因子为 ρ 的概率至少为逆拟多项式级别 Ω(exp(-c log²(ρ) log(n)))，这是高斯随机矩阵大增长概率的首个非平凡下界，并打破了平滑分析与平均情况分析中的标准猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带部分主元的高斯消元法（GEPP）的增长因子分析长期依赖平滑分析与平均情况分析中的标准猜想，但这些猜想缺乏对"大增长发生概率"的非平凡下界支撑，本文旨在给出该概率的首个非平凡下界并检验猜想的合理性。

Method: 针对 n×n 高斯随机矩阵上的带部分主元高斯消元过程，通过概率分析推导增长因子 ρ 出现概率的下界，得到形如 Ω(exp(-c log²(ρ) log(n))) 的逆拟多项式估计。

Result: 证明增长因子为 ρ 的概率至少为逆拟多项式级别 Ω(exp(-c log²(ρ) log(n)))（c>0 为常数）。该下界打破了平滑与平均情况分析中的标准猜想，且据作者所知，这是高斯随机矩阵大增长概率的首个非平凡下界。

Conclusion: GEPP 在随机矩阵上出现大增长的概率并非如标准猜想所假设的那样极低，而是至少达到逆拟多项式量级，这表明相关平滑与平均情况分析中的既有猜想需要修正。

Abstract: We prove that the probability that Gaussian elimination with partial pivoting on $n \times n$ random matrices has growth $ρ$ is at least inverse quasi-polynomial: $Ω(\exp(-c \log^2 (ρ)\log(n)))$ for some constant $c > 0$.
  This lower bound breaks standard conjectures in the smoothed and average-case analysis of Gaussian elimination.
  To the best of our knowledge, it is the first non-trivial lower bound on the probability of large growth for Gaussian random matrices.

</details>

### [84] [Encoding level-3 semi-directed phylogenetic networks by quarnets and quinnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07855)
*Niels Holtgrefe,Katharina T. Huber,Leo van Iersel,Vincent Moulton*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究半定向系统发育网络的编码问题，证明level-3半定向网络可被其5-net（quinnet）唯一编码，并精确刻画了quarnet编码的局限：单个已知反例是唯一障碍，其余level-3网络均可由其quarnet编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育网络可建模谱系的分化与融合，但由于遗传数据常无法确定根位置，只能推断半定向网络。常见推断策略是先推断各k叶集合诱导的子网络（k-net）再拼接，而这要求k-net能唯一确定原网络（即网络被其k-net编码）。已知level-1、level-2半定向网络可被4-net（quarnet）编码，但level-3网络不行，因此需要确定level-3网络的编码下限并刻画quarnet失败的精确范围。

Method: 作者从网络结构特征的层面入手，针对网络的各个具体结构特征（不限于固定level）建立了一系列编码结果，并利用这些结果分析level-3半定向网络的quinnet编码与quarnet编码。

Result: level-3半定向网络可被其5-net（quinnet）编码；对于quarnet，仅存在一个此前已报道的反例构成唯一障碍，除此之外所有其他level-3网络都能被其quarnet编码。

Conclusion: level-3半定向网络的编码由5-net保证，而对quarnet的局限性得到了精确刻画。所依赖的关于网络单个结构特征的编码结果适用于任意level，具有独立研究价值。

Abstract: Phylogenetic networks generalize phylogenetic trees as models of evolutionary history, allowing lineages to merge as well as to diverge. For many types of genetic data the root position of such a network cannot be recovered, so that only a semi-directed network can be inferred: a mixed graph in which only the edges entering a reticulation vertex are directed. A common strategy for inferring such a network is to first infer the subnetwork it induces on each set of $k\geq 3$ of its leaves, called a $k$-net, and then to assemble these pieces. This can only succeed if the $k$-nets determine the network, in which case that network is said to be encoded by its $k$-nets. Semi-directed networks of level-1 and 2, those whose biconnected components contain at most one, respectively two, reticulations, are known to be encoded by their $4$-nets, or quarnets, whereas level-3 networks are not. Even so, in this paper we show that level-3 semi-directed networks are encoded by their $5$-nets, or quinnets, and we characterize the limitation of quarnets exactly: we show that a single previously reported counterexample captures the only obstruction, every other level-3 network being encoded by its quarnets. Our proofs rest on a collection of encoding results for individual structural features of a network, which we establish for networks of arbitrary level and which are of independent interest.

</details>


### [85] [Effects of Periodic Migration on Selection in Subdivided Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08160)
*Mohammad Amin Masoumi,Kamran Kaveh,Mohammad Reza Ejtehadi*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该文利用双种群Moran模型研究周期性迁移对空间异质选择的影响，发现迁移周期性一般使种群趋向中性，且在反相迁移和方向不对称时效应最强，迁移周期响应呈非单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移与选择共同塑造空间结构种群的进化结果，而迁移可随生态变化（如海平面变动改变栖息地连通性）随时间波动。作者旨在探究周期性迁移如何影响地理细分种群中的选择过程。

Method: 采用双种群（two-deme）Moran模型，设定空间异质选择：突变基因型在一个栖息地有利、在另一栖息地不利。固定时间平均迁移率，变化迁移的周期、相对相位与方向不对称性，考察稳态突变频率。

Result: 迁移周期性通常驱动种群趋向中性：相对于恒定迁移，当突变体平均有利时降低其频率，平均有害时提高其频率，但在所考察参数区间内未逆转选择方向。反相迁移（模拟季节性迁移）效应最强，方向不对称进一步增强该效应。对迁移周期的响应总体呈非单调，同相迁移下存在使偏离恒定迁移行为最大的中间特征周期。机制在于Moran过程中迁移与局域选择的耦合：迁移的时间调制不仅改变个体交换，也改变选择所作用的局域种群组成。

Conclusion: 具有相同平均迁移率的种群，其进化结果可因基因流的时间组织方式不同而不同。

Abstract: Migration and selection jointly shape evolutionary outcomes in spatially structured populations. Migration can vary over time as ecological shifts, such as changes in sea level, alter habitat connectivity. Here, we study the impact of periodic migration on selection within a geographically subdivided population. Using a two-deme Moran model with spatially heterogeneous selection, we analyze a scenario in which a mutant genotype is advantageous in one habitat and disadvantageous in the other. Keeping the time-averaged migration rate fixed, we vary the period, relative phase, and directional asymmetry of migration and examine the resulting steady-state mutant frequency. We find that migration periodicity generally drives the population toward neutrality: relative to constant migration, it decreases the mutant frequency when mutants are advantageous on average and increases it when they are deleterious, without reversing the direction of selection in the parameter regimes examined. This effect is strongest for out-of-phase migration, which models seasonal migration, and is further enhanced by directional asymmetry. The response to the migration period is generally non-monotonic, with an intermediate characteristic period producing the largest deviation from constant-migration behavior under in-phase migration. These results arise from the coupling between migration and local selection in Moran process: temporal modulation of migration changes not only the exchange of individuals between habitats but also the local population composition on which selection acts. Our results show that populations with identical mean migration rates can show different evolutionary outcomes depending on the temporal organization of gene flow.

</details>

### [86] [Tight Bounds for Tusnády's Problem in the Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08130)
*Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了平面上 n 个点关于轴平行矩形的最坏情况组合偏差为 Θ(log^{3/2} n)，通过匹配下界完成紧确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知下界为 Ω(log n)、上界为 O(log^{3/2} n)，两者存在差距，研究者希望确定真实量级并证明匹配的下界。

Method: 证明简单初等：逐位揭示一个坐标；结合振荡势的增益与有界差分控制波动，并对所有着色做联合界。

Result: 证明了最坏情况下界为 Ω(log^{3/2} n)，从而总复杂度为 Θ(log^{3/2} n)；对随机点集也成立：以至少 1-e^{-An} 的概率，任意着色都存在锚定矩形偏差至少 c_A(log_2 n)^{3/2}。

Conclusion: 组合偏差的真实量级为 Θ(log^{3/2} n)，证明方法初等，揭示了逐位分析、振荡势与有界差分在偏差下界研究中的有效性。

Abstract: We show that the worst-case combinatorial discrepancy of $n$ points in the plane with respect to axis-parallel rectangles is $Θ(\log^{3/2}n)$. The known bounds were $Ω(\log n)$ and $O(\log^{3/2}n)$; we prove the matching lower bound. It holds for random point sets: for every $A>0$, there is a constant $c_A>0$ such that, with probability at least $1-e^{-An}$, every coloring of $n$ independent uniform points in the unit square has an anchored rectangle with imbalance at least $c_A(\log_2n)^{3/2}$.
  The proof is surprisingly simple and elementary. It reveals one coordinate digit by digit. With overwhelming probability over the points, the conditional gains of an oscillation potential add up to $Ω(\log^{3/2}n)$ over $Θ(\log n)$ digits. Bounded differences control the fluctuations well enough for a union bound over all colorings.

</details>


### [87] [Exposition of an approximation algorithm for mixed volumes of a constant number of convex bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08248)
*Hariharan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定数量凸体混合体积的相对近似问题，提出了一个使用多项式次成员oracle调用和比特运算的随机算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合体积是凸几何中的核心量，但精确计算困难。作者希望在给定成员oracle和包含边界条件下，实现混合体积的高效相对误差近似。

Method: 针对$\mathbb{R}^n$中固定数量$k$个满维凸体的混合体积，在成员oracle和已知界限$B_n\subseteq K_i\subseteq R_0B_n$下，设计随机算法估计任意指定混合体积，相对误差为$\varepsilon$。

Result: 算法以至少$1-\delta$的概率将估计误差控制在相对误差$\varepsilon$以内，且oracle调用次数和比特运算量在$n$、$\log R_0$、$\varepsilon^{-1}$、$\log\delta^{-1}$上均为多项式（固定$k$时）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了混合体积相对近似的一个高效随机算法，复杂度关于相关参数为多项式级别。

Abstract: We study $\varepsilon$-relative approximation of mixed volumes of a fixed number $k$ of full-dimensional convex bodies in $\mathbb{R}^n$, given membership oracles and a known bound $B_n\subseteq K_i\subseteq R_0B_n$. We present a randomized algorithm that estimates any prescribed mixed volume within relative error $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, using polynomially many oracle calls and bit operations in $n$, $\log R_0$, $\varepsilon^{-1}$, and $\logδ^{-1}$ for fixed $k$.

</details>


### [88] [Simulated Annealing for Antenna Placements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08338)
*Mikkel Abrahamsen,Jacobus Conradi,Asbjørn Lind*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 针对2026 ACM SIGSPATIAL GIS Cup的几何覆盖优化问题，提出将可见性预处理与增量式组合搜索组合的求解框架，在9组参数下覆盖261至16,273栋建筑，并入选顶级方案受邀报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛要求在给定互不相交的简单多边形集合上，于多边形边界上放置k个天线（点），使"周长可见比例不低于τ"的多边形数量最大化，限时24小时，需在有限时间内获得尽可能优的解，因此需要兼顾几何计算效率与组合搜索质量。

Method: 将几何可见性预处理与组合搜索分离：先做可见性预处理，再以"构造池"生成天线限制在多边形顶点上的初始解；主搜索采用模拟退火进行天线一对一替换，并辅以精确离散单交换下降、ruin-and-recreate（破坏—重建）与精英交叉；设置次级目标奖励向服务阈值τ的推进但不覆盖主评分；最后将候选天线扩展到多边形边内部点。

Result: 提交的解覆盖9组参数组合，建筑数量从261到16,273不等；团队作为顶级参赛方案之一受邀进行报告，代码已开源（https://github.com/JacobusTheSecond/giscup）。

Conclusion: 预处理与元启发式/组合搜索分层的设计能有效应对大规模边界点连续选址问题，顶点解作为搜索起点、边内部点扩展可进一步提升解质量，该框架在限时竞赛中表现具有竞争力。

Abstract: The 2026 ACM SIGSPATIAL GIS Cup posed the following geometric challenge: Given a set of simple, pair-wise disjoint polygons, compute $k$ antennas (points) on polygon boundaries, maximizing the number of polygons with at least a fraction $τ$ of their perimeter visible from the antennas. Contestants had 24 hours to produce the best possible solutions.
  We describe an approach that separates geometric visibility preprocessing from a portfolio of incremental combinatorial searches. A construction pool provides initial solutions with antennas restricted to polygon vertices; simulated annealing explores one-for-one antenna swaps, complemented by exact discrete one-swap descent, ruin-and-recreate, and elite crossover. A secondary objective rewards progress toward the service threshold without overriding the primary score. Candidate antennas are later expanded to include points in polygon-edge interiors. Our submitted solutions cover between 261 and 16,273 buildings across the nine parameter combinations, and the team was invited to present as one of the top entries. The code is available at https://github.com/JacobusTheSecond/giscup.

</details>

### [89] [Densities of Picard Rank Jumps for K3 Surfaces Over Global Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06888)
*Robin Huang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文给出了代数K3曲面在归约处几何Néron-Severi群秩的素数密度精确公式，并研究了固定平方类元素出现的无穷多归约及其密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究K3曲面归约的几何Néron-Severi群秩分布是算术几何中的重要问题。作者希望用附着于X的Galois表示来刻画秩为给定值的素数密度，并进一步理解几何Néron-Severi群中固定平方类元素出现的归约行为。

Method: 设X为整体域K（特征0或p≥3，函数域情形假定ordinary）上的代数K3曲面。利用附着于X的Galois表示，推导几何Néron-Severi群秩为给定值的素数密度精确公式。作为推广，证明存在无穷多归约使得几何Néron-Severi群含有在相交配对下表示固定平方类的元素，并研究此类位置的密度。

Result: 得到了几何Néron-Severi群秩给定值的素数密度精确公式；证明了无穷多归约含有表示固定平方类的几何Néron-Severi群元素，并给出了相应密度的结果。特别地，当X对应充分一般的模点（moduli point）时，满足该条件的位置集合密度为零。

Conclusion: 该工作通过Galois表示建立了K3曲面归约几何Néron-Severi群秩与素数的精确密度联系，并刻画了固定平方类元素出现归约的密度；在充分一般模点情形下该密度为零。

Abstract: Let $X$ be an algebraic K3 surface defined over a global field $K$ of characteristic $0$ or $p \geq 3$, assumed ordinary in the case where $K$ is a global function field. We provide an exact formula for the density of primes $p$ of $K$ at which the geometric Neron-Severi group of the reduction $X_p$ has a given rank in terms of the Galois representation attached to $X$. As an extension, we prove that there are infinitely many reductions of $X$ with an element of the geometric Neron-Severi group representing a fixed square class under the intersection pairing and prove results about the density of such places. In particular, we show that if $X$ corresponds to a sufficiently generic moduli point, a density zero set of places of $K$ satisfy this condition.

</details>


### [90] [CUDA-MPQS: A GPU-Resident Self-Initializing Quadratic Sieve, and the Factorization of RSA-155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07126)
*Fabian Januszewski,Christoph Heinrichs*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 提出全GPU自初始化二次筛法SW，除编排与IO外所有阶段均在GPU执行，端到端分解RSA-155，为QS当前最大整数分解记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次筛法(Qs)分支密集、访存不规则且对缓存不友好，已有GPU工作仅加速其中单个阶段；作者希望将QS全流程GPU化并推进QS可分解规模的上限。

Method: 自初始化二次筛法，所有阶段——多项式初始化、筛法、关系后处理、大素数匹配、GF(2)矩阵构造、Block Wiedemann线性代数与平方根——全部在GPU执行，CPU仅负责编排、初始化和I/O；筛窗99.9%时间GPU繁忙，无设备级或事件同步、无同步拷贝。

Result: 端到端分解512位RSA-155：64×H100筛法+单H100 GF(2)求解，共700.6 GPU-h、242 kWh，其中98.4%为筛法。单卡H100分解RSA-100需29.2 s，消费级RTX 5070 Ti需51.0 s；同模数对比下H100快于96核EPYC 9655上的YAFU(106–122 s)、CADO-NFS的SIQS(292 s)与GNFS(263 s)。四种GPU的RSA-100吞吐近似与CUDA核心数成正比，显存带宽预测性较弱；RSA-100至RSA-155的七次基准分解成本按L^{1/2}[1]^{1.04}增长(R^2=0.993)。RSA-150上测得302.9 GPU-h，对比此前记录的11,664 core-hours。

Conclusion: 该工作将二次筛法完整GPU化，端到端分解RSA-155，超越此前QS记录RSA-150(YAFU, 2025)；近三十年所有可比的一般整数分解(含1999年的RSA-155)均使用数域筛法，本工作以QS达成更大规模，且GPU成本增长近似L^{1/2}[1]定律。

Abstract: The quadratic sieve (QS) is an irregular, cache-hostile, branch-heavy integer factorization algorithm; prior GPU work accelerates individual stages of it. We present \swname{}, a self-initializing quadratic sieve in which \emph{every} stage --- polynomial initialization, sieving, relation post-processing, large-prime matching, \GFtwo{} matrix construction, Block Wiedemann linear algebra and the square root --- executes on the GPU, the CPU restricted to orchestration, setup and I/O, the sieve window 99.9\% GPU-busy with no device-wide or event synchronization and no synchronous copy. With it we factored the 512-bit RSA-155 challenge modulus end to end on GPUs: a 64$\times$H100 sieve feeding a single-H100 \GFtwo{} solve, 700.6~GPU-h and 242~kWh in total, 98.4\% of it sieving. To our knowledge this is the largest integer factored by the quadratic sieve, exceeding the prior QS record RSA-150 (Buhrow, YAFU, 2025); all comparable general factorizations since 1996, RSA-155's own in 1999 included, used the number field sieve. On a single device we factor RSA-100 in 29.2~s on an H100 and 51.0~s on a consumer RTX~5070~Ti; in a controlled same-modulus measurement, with energy measured on both sides, the H100 is faster than the fastest CPU quadratic sieve, YAFU, on 96 cores of an EPYC~9655 (106--122~s), and than CADO-NFS's SIQS (292~s) and GNFS (263~s). Across four discrete GPUs (a 3.7$\times$ range in CUDA cores) RSA-100 throughput is close to proportional to core count, memory bandwidth being a weaker predictor, and measured cost over seven benchmark factorizations from RSA-100 to RSA-155 grows as $\LN{1/2}{1}^{1.04}$ ($R^2=0.993$). On RSA-150, the identical modulus on which the prior QS record was set, we measure 302.9~GPU-h against that run's 11{,}664 core-hours.

</details>


### [91] [On the class number of real multiquadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07180)
*Mohamed Mahmoud Chems-Eddin,Hamza El Mamry*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将实二次域与双二次域类数分类结果推广：完整分类2-类数为2的实双二次域，并给出所有奇类数的实多二次域列表，附录给出√ε_d的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conner与Hurrelbrink（1988）已分类奇类数的实二次域和双二次域，并研究2-类数为2的实二次域；结合Kučera的结果，2-类数为2的实二次域已有完整分类。但2-类数为2的实双二次域以及一般实多二次域的奇类数分类尚不完整，本文旨在补全这些缺口。

Method: 在已有实二次域分类基础上，通过类群与2-类群的理论分析，对实双二次域2-类数为2的情形进行完整分类；并系统地列举奇类数的实多二次域；附录中推导√ε_d的显式表达式，其中ε_d为Q(√d)的基本单位。

Result: 给出了2-类数为2的所有实双二次域的完整分类；给出了所有奇类数实多二次域的完整列表；附录提供了√ε_d的显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文补全了实双二次域2-类数为2及实多二次域奇类数的分类，并附带基本单位平方根的显式公式，为相关数域类数问题提供了完整结果。

Abstract: In their 1988 monograph, Conner and Hurrelbrink classified all real quadratic and biquadratic fields with odd class number. They also investigated real quadratic fields whose $2$-class numbers are equal to $2$. Their results, together with some well-known results of Ku{č}era, provide a complete classification of real quadratic number fields with $2$-class number $2$.
  The main aim of this paper is to provide a complete classification of all real biquadratic fields whose $2$-class numbers equal $2$; and moreover, we draw the complete list all real multiquadratic fields with odd class numbers. Finally, as an appendix we give explicit formulas for $\sqrt{\varepsilon_d}$, where $\varepsilon_d$ denotes the fundamental unit of the real quadratic field $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{d})$.

</details>


### [92] [The One-Power Erdős Conjecture for Primes $p \equiv 7 \pmod{36}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07214)
*Shen Harman,Dana Paquin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Erdős猜想（每个奇数n>1可写为n=2^a+m，m无平方因子）在素数类p≡7 (mod 36)上的情形，利用分离原理将四元多项式的平方因子问题化为独立线性问题，无条件证明a≤3可表示素数具有相对密度1-ρ₄（ρ₄≈1.86×10⁻⁵），并计算验证10⁸以下该类素数猜想成立且a≤5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős猜想断言每个奇数n>1都能表示为n=2^a+m，其中m为正的无平方因子整数。该猜想整体上很难处理，作者希望在特定素数类p≡7 (mod 36)上取得进展：因为此时候选余数p-1、p-2、p-4、p-8中素因子2和3可被完全控制，从而把无平方因子性障碍限制在q≥5的素数上，使问题更易分析。同时借助Hercher引理的误差界修正，完善相关工具。

Method: 令x=(p-1)/6，则四个候选余数分别为6x、6x-1、3(2x-1)、6x-7，其中2和3的贡献完全可控，无平方因子障碍仅来自q≥5。对q≥5建立分离原理：由于任意两余数之差至多7<25，故q²至多整除其中一个余数，且在宽度小于20的指数窗口中仍保持分离。由此把四元多项式x(6x-1)(2x-1)(6x-7)的平方除子问题分解为相互独立的线性问题，并计算显式的Euler型常数ρ₄。此外，通过计算搜索验证10⁸以下的素数。

Result: 无条件证明p≡7 (mod 36)中可用a≤3表示的素数具有相对密度1-ρ₄，其中ρ₄=1.8637×10⁻⁵为显式Euler型常数；由此推出该类素数中有无穷多个不能用a≤3表示。计算上验证了10⁸以下该类素数均满足猜想，且所需指数始终a≤5，并具体列出10⁸以下需要a=5的十一个素数。附录修正了Hercher某引理中的误差界，但结论不变。

Conclusion: 在素数类p≡7 (mod 36)上，分离原理将Erdős猜想的无平方因子问题约化为独立线性问题，从而给出a≤3可表示素数的密度定量结果及无穷多个反例的存在性；计算验证支持猜想的正确性（10⁸以下全部成立，a≤5）。附录修正了Hercher引理的误差界，且不影响其结论。

Abstract: A conjecture of Erdős asserts that every odd integer $n > 1$ can be written as $n = 2^a + m$, where $a \ge 0$ is an integer and $m$ is a squarefree positive integer. We study this conjecture for primes $p \equiv 7 \pmod{36}$. Writing $x = (p-1)/6$, the candidate remainders take the shapes $p - 1 = 6x$, $p-2 = 6x-1$, $p-4 = 3(2x-1)$, and $p-8 = 6x-7$, in which the primes $2$ and $3$ are completely controlled: every obstruction to squarefreeness comes from a prime $q \ge 5$. For such $q$ a separation principle applies --- $q^2$ divides at most one of the remainders, since two of them differ by at most $7 < 25$ --- and it persists across any window of exponents of width less than $20$. Separation collapses the square-divisor problem for the quartic $x(6x-1)(2x-1)(6x-7)$ into independent linear problems, and we deduce unconditionally that the primes of the class representable with $a \le 3$ have relative density $1 - ρ_4$, where $ρ_4 = 1.8637 \times 10^{-5}$ is an explicit Euler-type constant; in particular, infinitely many primes of the class are not so representable. Computationally, we verify the conjecture for all primes of the class below $10^8$, always with $a \le 5$, and we exhibit the eleven primes below $10^8$ requiring $a = 5$. An appendix corrects the error bound in a lemma of Hercher, whose conclusions are unaffected.

</details>


### [93] [Systems of equations with elements of finitely generated groups and with linear recurrence sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07227)
*Subham Bhakta,Alina Ostafe,Igor E. Shparlinski*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对有限生成群有限子集中对角线多项式方程组（k个方程、s个变量，特征零域）的解数给出新的本质最优上界，推广并部分改进了k=1、k=2的已有结果，并据此改进了矩阵算术统计的结论；同时将类似结果推广到线性递推序列组的线性与二次方程情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于对角线方程组解数上界的结果主要针对k=1（Bourgain、Garaev、Konyagin、Shparlinski，2014）和k=2（Manning、Ostafe、Shparlinski，2026），缺乏对一般k及递推序列情形的本质最优估计，且k=2结果限制了矩阵算术统计的精度。

Method: 研究有限生成群有限子集上、特征零域中k个多项式方程、s个变量的对角线方程组，建立解数上界；并采用平行方法处理s元线性递推序列组的线性方程与二次方程。

Result: 得到对角线方程组解数的新的本质最优上界，推广并部分改进k=1与k=2的既有结果；由此改进Manning、Ostafe、Shparlinski（2026）关于矩阵算术统计的结果；并对递推序列组的线性、二次方程得到类似上界。

Conclusion: 研究将对角线方程组解数上界统一推广到一般k，并延伸至递推序列情形，同时增强矩阵算术统计的应用结论。

Abstract: We obtain new and essentially optimal bounds on the number of solutions of diagonal systems of $k$ polynomial equations in $s$ variables from an arbitrary finite subset of a finitely generated group in a field of characteristic zero. Our bounds generalise and sometimes improve previous results of J. Bourgain, M. Z. Garaev, S. V. Konyagin, and I. E. Shparlinski (2014), for $k=1$, and of A. Manning, A. Ostafe and I. E. Shparlinski (2026), for $k=2$. In turn, this improvement leads to sharpening a result of A. Manning, A. Ostafe and I. E. Shparlinski (2026) concerning arithmetic statistics of certain matrices.
  We also obtain similar results for $s$-tuples of linear recurrence sequences, in the case of linear equations and quadratic equations.

</details>

### [95] [Lifting ethereal Bianchi modular forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07445)
*Lewis Combes,Håvard Damm-Johnsen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究模 \(p\) Bianchi 模形式的提升性问题，猜测通过提高 level 总可实现到特征零的提升，并开发上同调退化映射算法进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模 \(p\) Hecke 特征形式（对 \(\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})\) 同余子群）中不能提升到特征零的情形受到广泛关注，G. Schaeffer 将其命名为“ethereal modular forms”。本文希望将这一提升性问题推广到模 \(p\) Bianchi 模形式，探究是否类似经典模形式可通过提高 level 实现提升。

Method: 开发了计算上同调中退化映射（degeneracy maps）的高效算法，用于严格验证特征值同余；对小素数和特定域，利用 Caraiani–Newton 最近的模性定理证明提升始终存在；并结合 Magma 实现与 Cremona 提供的 LMFDB 数据进行分析。

Result: 计算表明模 \(p\) Bianchi 模形式可能总能通过提高 level 提升到特征零；在有利参数范围内，Magma 实现与 LMFDB 数据显示大多数模 \(p\) Bianchi 模形式可以提升。

Conclusion: 模 \(p\) Bianchi 模形式的提升性行为与经典模形式类似，提高 level 可能保证特征零提升的存在，且在部分情形下可由模性定理严格证明。

Abstract: Mod $p$ Hecke eigenforms for congruence subgroups of $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ which do not lift to characteristic $0$ have received much attention, and were named ethereal modular forms by G. Schaeffer. In this paper, we study liftability for mod $p$ Bianchi modular forms: computations suggest that a lift to characteristic zero may always be possible by increasing the level, as in the case of classical modular forms.
  We develop efficient algorithms for computing degeneracy maps in cohomology, which allow us to rigorously verify eigenvalue congruences. For small primes and certain fields, we also prove that lifts always exist as a consequence of the recent modularity theorem due to Caraiani-Newton. A Magma implementation, complemented by LMFDB data due to Cremona, shows that in favourable parameter ranges, most mod $p$ Bianchi modular forms lift.

</details>


### [96] [Local newforms, Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker functions, and explicit test vectors for $\mathrm{GL}(n)\times \mathrm{GL}(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08633)
*Alexandros Groutides*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker函数与Whittaker新形式之间的逆向平均公式，从而建立了两者的双向显式转换，并给出了GL(n)×GL(n) Rankin-Selberg L-因子的新显式测试向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Girsch-Kurinczuk近期获得了p进GL(n)中depth-zero和minimax integral-depth尖点表示局部新形式的公式，其核心结果是给出Whittaker新形式关于Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker函数的积分表示。作者希望补全这一对应关系的另一方向，实现两向量之间的双向显式联系。

Method: 通过建立Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker函数关于Whittaker新形式的积分表示，证明逆向平均公式；并结合该平均公式与Kurinczuk-Matringe的先前工作构造测试向量。

Result: 证明了逆向平均公式，与Girsch-Kurinczuk的公式合起来给出两个distinguished向量之间的显式双向通道；并得到GL(n)×GL(n)尖点对的显式测试向量（以其Rankin-Selberg L-因子不全等于1为条件）。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了局部新形式理论中Whittaker新形式与Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker函数的相互表示，并为Rankin-Selberg L-因子提供了新的显式测试向量。

Abstract: Recently, Girsch-Kurinczuk have obtained new formulas for local newforms in depth-zero and minimax integral-depth cuspidal representations of $p$-adic $\mathrm{GL}(n)$. One of their main results is an averaging formula which provides an integral representation of the Whittaker newform in terms of the Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker function. In the present paper, we first prove a reverse averaging formula by establishing an integral representation of the Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker function in terms of the Whittaker newform. Together, these formulas give an explicit two-way passage between these two distinguished vectors. Finally, we use our averaging formula and earlier work of Kurinczuk-Matringe to give new explicit test vectors in terms of Whittaker newforms for such cuspidal $\mathrm{GL}(n)\times\mathrm{GL}(n)$ pairs, whenever their Rankin-Selberg $L$-factor is not identically equal to $1$.

</details>


### [97] [Gaps theorems for linear forms with cubic coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07471)
*Alan Haynes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究三次数域中两参数分数部分的间隙个数 $G(\mathbf{α},\mathbf{τ})$，证明其随 $\mathbf{τ}$ 无界；全实情形由 Shapira 对角线轨道稠密性定理推出，复嵌入情形给出显式构造并得到对数幂次下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维分数部分（多维 Kronecker 序列）的间隙分布是 Diophantine 逼近与齐性动力学的核心问题；对三次数域的两参数情形，间隙个数的无界性此前尚不清楚，本文旨在揭示数与动力学结构之间的联系。

Method: 设 $1,α_1,α_2$ 为实三次数域 $K$ 的一组基，定义 $G(\mathbf{α},\mathbf{τ})$ 为 $\{m_1α_1+m_2α_2\}$（$0\le m_i<τ_i$）分数部分的相异间隙数。全实情形借助 Shapira 关于对角群在格空间上轨道稠密性的定理进行推导；含复嵌入情形则采用显式构造方法。

Result: 证明 $G(\mathbf{α},\mathbf{τ})$ 随 $\mathbf{τ}$ 变化时无界；并且在 $K$ 含复嵌入时，显式构造给出常数 $c,κ>0$，使对无穷多 $\mathbf{τ}\in\mathbb{N}^2$ 成立 $G(\mathbf{α},\mathbf{τ})\ge c(\log\max\{τ_1,τ_2\})^κ$。

Conclusion: 三次数域上两参数分数部分的间隙个数无界；复嵌入情形下还可给出对数幂次量级的下界，表明间隙结构随截断参数以至少对数幂次增长，并把该问题与轨迹稠密性等齐性动力学结果相联系。

Abstract: Let $1,α_1,α_2$ be a basis for a cubic number field $K\subset\mathbb{R}$, and for $τ_1,τ_2\ge 1$ let $G(\mathbfα,\mathbfτ)$ denote the number of distinct gaps between the fractional parts of the numbers $m_1α_1+m_2α_2$, with $m_1,m_2\in\mathbb{Z}$, $0\le m_1<τ_1$ and $0\le m_2<τ_2$. We show that $G(\mathbfα,\mathbfτ)$ is unbounded as $\mathbfτ$ varies. When $K$ is totally real this is deduced from a density theorem of Shapira for orbits of the diagonal group on the space of lattices. When $K$ has a complex embedding we give an explicit construction, which also shows that there are constants $c,κ>0$ for which $G(\mathbfα,\mathbfτ)\ge c(\log\max\{τ_1,τ_2\})^κ$ for infinitely many $\mathbfτ\in\mathbb{N}^2$.

</details>


### [98] [Further results on the paramodular Hecke algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07520)
*Jennifer Johnson-Leung,Joshua Parker,Brooks Roberts*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究分次paramodular Hecke代数 $\mathcal H$ 的结构，定义其被某自同构 $\alpha$ 固定的交换子环 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$，证明由 $k$ 次双陪集之和定义的 $T(q^k)$ 属于 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$，并给出生成函数的有理表达式及 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的结构与关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解分次paramodular Hecke代数 $\mathcal H$ 的结构，通过引入自同构不动子环 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 来刻画 $\mathcal H$ 的交换部分及其与整体的关系。

Method: 定义 $\mathcal H$ 中被自同构 $\alpha$ 固定的元素构成的交换子环 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$；考虑 $k$ 次双陪集之和 $T(q^k)$，证明其属于 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$；研究以 $T(q^k)$ 为系数的形式幂级数的有理性质，并确定 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的结构及其与 $\mathcal H$ 的关系。

Result: 证明 $T(q^k)\in\mathcal{H}^\alpha$；证明以 $t$ 为不定元、系数为 $T(q^k)$ 的形式幂级数可表示为系数取自 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的关于 $t$ 的显式有理函数；确定 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的结构及其与 $\mathcal H$ 的关系。

Conclusion: 分次paramodular Hecke代数含有一个由自同构 $\alpha$ 固定的交换子环 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$，双陪集和 $T(q^k)$ 均落入其中，且其生成函数具有显式有理形式，从而揭示了 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的结构及其与 $\mathcal H$ 的联系。

Abstract: In this paper we further examine the structure of the graded paramodular Hecke algebra $\mathcal H$. We define a commutative subring $\mathcal{H}^α$ of $\mathcal{H}$ as the elements fixed by a certain automorphism $α$, and we show that the elements $T(q^k)$, defined as the sum of the double cosets of degree $k$, are contained in $\mathcal{H}^α$. We prove that the formal power series in the indeterminate $t$ with coefficients $T(q^k)$ is an explicit rational function in $t$ with coefficients from $\mathcal{H}^α$. We also determine the structure of $\mathcal{H}^α$ and its relation to $\mathcal{H}$.

</details>


### [99] [Primitive normal progressions in finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07538)
*Gustav Kjærbye Bagger,Matthew Fernando*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究有限域 $\mathbb{F}_{q^n}$ 中长度 $m$ 的等差数列，要求各项均为本原元且至少一项为正规元；综合筛法估计、分歧界与计算论证，显著改进 $m=4$ 的结果，给出 $5\le m\le 10$ 的部分结果，并提供在 $m\ge 53$ 时优于既有文献的一般界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限域中同时具有本原性与正规性的元素（本原正规元）是代数与编码理论中的重要研究对象，将其推广到等差数列结构有助于理解有限域乘法群结构及其在序列、密码与编码中的应用；已有文献对等差列中本原正规元的存在性结果在长度 $m=4$ 及中等长度区间仍较弱，需要改进。

Method: 结合筛法估计（用于计数满足本原性与正规性条件的元素）、分歧界（ramified bounds，处理 $q^n-1$ 与 $x^n-1$ 等因式分解相关的特征和/素因子估计）以及计算论证（对小参数情形进行验证或补足）。

Result: 对 $m=4$ 显著超越此前文献的结论；对 $5\le m\le 10$ 得到部分结果（即在某些 $q,n$ 条件下保证存在性）；并给出一个在 $m\ge 53$ 范围内优于以往结果的一般性界。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了有限域等差列中本原正规元存在性问题的研究，既在短长度 $m=4$ 上取得显著改进，又在中等长度提供部分进展，并在长长度 $m\ge 53$ 上给出更优的一般界，显示筛法与分歧界结合方法的有效性。

Abstract: Consider an arithmetic progression inside the finite field $\mathbb{F}_{q^n}$. We study the existence of progressions whose terms are all primitive and at least one of them is normal. Using a combination of sieve estimates, ramified bounds, and computational arguments, we improve significantly upon previous literature for progressions of length $m=4$ and obtain partial results whenever $5\leq m\leq 10$. Finally, we provide a general bound which outperforms previous results in the range $m\geq 53$.

</details>


### [100] [A new bound for the Furstenberg--Sárközy theorem using the van der Corput property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07765)
*Steve Fan,Andrew Lott*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了无平方差的集合大小上界为 |A| ≪ N exp(-c√(log N log log N))，改进了 Green–Sawhney 的结果，并给出匹配的下界说明该定量 van der Corput 界在常数意义下是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究整数区间 [1,N] 中不含非零平方差的集合 A 的极大尺寸问题，即对 Roth 型/平方差问题的定量改进，此前 Green 与 Sawhney 已给出相关上界，本文旨在改进该上界并确认所用量化工具的紧性。

Method: 利用带符号系数的 van der Corput 性质的定量版本，并基于 Slijepčević、Slijepčević–Ninčević 以及 Fan–Lott 的既有构造进行发展；上界证明为初等且自洽。同时对平方数的任意 van der Corput 见证的常系数给出匹配下界。

Result: 得到上界 |A| ≪ N exp(-c√(log N log log N))，改进了 Green–Sawhney 的结果；并证明了定量 van der Corput 界在常数 c 意义下是尖锐的（下界与上界匹配）。

Conclusion: 无平方差集合的尺寸上界被改进为次幂型指数衰减形式，且所依赖的定量 van der Corput 方法在常数层面达到最优；证明初等自洽，方法可推广至相关加性组合问题。

Abstract: We show that if $A\subseteq \mathbb{N}\cap[1,N]$ has no nonzero square difference, then \[
  |A|\ll N\exp(-c\sqrt{\log N\log\log N}), \] improving upon a recent result of Green and Sawhney. The proof exploits a quantitative version of the van der Corput property with signed coefficients and builds on previous constructions of Slijepčević, Slijepčević--Ninčević, and Fan-Lott. The proof of the upper bound is elementary and self-contained. We also prove a matching lower bound for the constant coefficient of any van der Corput witness for squares, showing that our quantitative van der Corput bound is sharp up to the constant $c$.

</details>


### [101] [Carlet's cyclic-additive conjecture for the Kasami monomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07905)
*Gábor P. Nagy,Douglas S. McNeil,Attila Vajda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明Kasami函数的导数像集合满足Carlet提出的循环-加性差集条件，对任意允许的(n,k)均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kasami函数在NSUCRYPTO 2019上被提出，其导数像集合Δ_k是否满足Carlet引入的循环-加性差集条件此前未解决，该条件与函数的密码学性质相关。

Method: 从导数像的half-size性质出发，将Fourier修正项表示为扭曲根计数，并通过Fermat三次曲线上的关联（incidence）论证证明所需的非负性；随后对斜率做精确平均从而逐点推出等式。论证已在Lean 4与Mathlib中形式化并机器验证。

Result: 对任意互异非零v1,v2∈K，证明|{(x,y,z)∈Δ_k^3 : v1x+v2y+(v1+v2)z=0}|=2^{2n-3}，对所有允许的(n,k)成立。

Conclusion: Kasami函数满足Carlet的循环-加性差集条件，解决了NSUCRYPTO 2019提出的问题，且证明经Lean 4形式化验证。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a finite field of characteristic two with $|K| = 2^{n}$, let $\gcd(k,n) = 1$, let $d_{k} = 4^{k} - 2^{k} + 1$ be the Kasami exponent, and let $Δ_{k} = \{(b+1)^{d_{k}} + b^{d_{k}} + 1 : b \in K\}$ be the image of the normalised derivative of the Kasami monomial in the direction $1$. We show that, for all distinct nonzero $v_{1},v_{2} \in K$, \[
  \bigl|\{(x,y,z) \in Δ_{k}^{3} :
  v_{1}x + v_{2}y + (v_{1}+v_{2})z = 0\}\bigr| = 2^{2n-3}. \] This establishes the cyclic-additive difference-set condition introduced by Carlet and later posed for the Kasami functions at NSUCRYPTO~2019. Starting from the known half-size property of the derivative image, we express the Fourier correction as twisted root counts and prove their required nonnegativity by an incidence argument on the Fermat cubic. An exact average over the slopes then forces equality pointwise. The argument covers every admissible pair $(n,k)$ and has been formalised and machine-checked in Lean~4 with Mathlib.

</details>


### [102] [A concise proof of the 8 case of Sylvester's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08015)
*Hongbo Yin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出Sylvester猜想中 $p\\equiv 8 \\bmod 9$ 情形的更简洁直接的证明，通过深挖作者此前工作并结合模函数工具，避免了Gross-Zagier公式等复杂手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sylvester猜想（150年前提出）断言每个满足 $p\\equiv 4,7,8 \\bmod 9$ 的素数可表示为两个有理立方数之和。作者已证明4、7情形，Burungale-Tian证明了8情形但其证明依赖作者结果却未充分利用，且复杂难懂，因此需要更简洁直接的证明。

Method: 深挖作者在文献\\cite{Yin}中的结果，结合其在8情形前期工作\\cite{Yin8}的思想，使用常迹（ordinary trace）和模函数的Kronecker同余，而不使用Gross-Zagier公式、$\\sqrt{-3}$-除子边界和Fermat同源。

Result: 得到 $p\\equiv 8 \\bmod 9$ 情形的更简洁直接的证明，为该猜想的所有情形提供了统一而简化的处理。

Conclusion: 该简洁证明为这一古老猜想画上完美句号。

Abstract: The 150 years old Sylvester's conjecture asserts that every prime $p\equiv 4,7,8\mod 9$ is the sum of two rational cubes. Recently, a proof of this old problem is obtained by the work of mine for the case 4,7 \cite{Yin} and the work of Burungale-Tian for the case 8 \cite{BT}. Burungale-Tian's paper essentially uses the results of paper \cite{Yin}, but does not make full use of the power. Their proof for the case $8$ is complicated and hard to follow. In this paper we give a much simpler and direct proof by deep mining my result in \cite{Yin} combining with the ideas of my previous work on case 8 in \cite{Yin8}. In particular, we use the ordinary trace and the Kronecker congruence of modular functions, but do not use the Gross-Zagier formula, $\sqrt{-3}$-division boundary and Fermat isogeny as in \cite{BT}.This concise proof provides a perfect end to this old concise conjecture.

</details>


### [103] [The Artin-Davenport conjecture on cubic forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08226)
*Dante Bonolis,Tim Browning,Jakob Glas,Victor Y. Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了任意十个及以上变量的整系数三次型必有非平凡整数零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广并改进该主题上已有作者关于整系数三次型非平凡整数零点的结果，将变量数下界降低至十个。

Method: 综合运用几何方法与圆法：部分情形用几何方法构造零点，其余情形用圆法处理。几何方面引入奇异轨迹与关联簇维数的界；解析方面引入分层几何筛法、有限域上点计数的分层结果以及立方根 Hensel 提升分析。

Result: 证明任意十个或更多变量的整系数三次型均存在非平凡整数零点。

Conclusion: 在几何与解析两方面的新工具（维数界、分层几何筛法、Hensel 提升分析）共同作用下，成功将三次型非平凡整数零点问题的变量数下界推进到十个。

Abstract: We show that any integral cubic form in ten or more variables has a nontrivial integer zero. Like earlier authors on this topic, we use geometric methods to produce a zero in some cases, and the circle method in all other cases. New ingredients are required on both fronts: in the first case, bounds on the dimension of singular loci and incidence varieties, and in the second case, a stratified geometric sieve, together with stratification results for point counts over finite fields and a cube-root Hensel lifting analysis.

</details>


### [104] [The average number of representations of an integer as a sum of three or more primes, over multiples of a fixed integer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08285)
*Alessandra Migliaccio,Alessandro Zaccagnini*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 将 Ikeda–Suriajaya (2025) 的结果推广到任意 $j\ge 3$ 个素数之和的表示数均值，并证明一般情形下的渐近公式成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果仅针对特定个数的素数之和的表示数均值，作者希望将其推广到任意 $j\ge 3$ 的一般情形。

Method: 引入新工具，例如广义 Goldbach 问题奇异级数的均值估计。

Result: 证明了任意 $j\ge 3$ 个素数之和表示数均值的渐近公式在一般情形下成立。

Conclusion: 该工作把 Ikeda–Suriajaya (2025) 的结果完整推广到任意素数个数情形，并借助奇异级数均值等新成分完成证明。

Abstract: We extend a result by Ikeda and Suriajaya (2025) to the average number of representations of an integer as a sum of an arbitrary number $j\ge 3$ of primes. We show that the corresponding asymptotic formula holds in the general case. In the proof we use new ingredients like averages for the singular series of the generalized Goldbach problem.

</details>


### [105] [Perfect powers with few digits and $S$-unit coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08310)
*Darsana N,Parvathi S Nair,Sudhansu Sekhar Rout*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究 S-单位方程中完美幂的有限性及完美幂稀疏表示问题，证明相关方程解集有限，并拓展五位数表示下完美 q 次幂不存在的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Corvaja-Zannier 已证明二进制中恰有四个非零数字的奇数完美幂有限，Bennett-Bugeaud 研究了稀疏表示，本文旨在推广这些有限性与稀疏表示结果。

Method: 结合子空间定理关于 S-单位点处解析级数整值性的推论与数域上的 Roth 定理；并利用阿基米德与非阿基米德对数线性形式的显式下界。

Result: 证明了 S-单位方程 y^d=1+c_1g^{m_1}+c_2g^{m_2}+c_3g^{m_3} 解集有限；并证明对固定基 g≥2，存在 q_0(g)，使任意素数 q>q_0(g)，不存在完美 q 次幂在基 g 下具有五个非零数字的表示。

Conclusion: 推广了 Corvaja-Zannier 与 Bennett-Bugeaud 的相关结果，深化了对 S-单位方程完美幂及完美幂稀疏表示的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we study two questions concerning perfect powers in $S$-unit equations and sparse representations of perfect powers. Corvaja-Zannier \cite{corvaja2013finiteness} proved that there are only finitely many odd perfect powers in $\N$ having precisely four non-zero digits in their binary expansion. At first, we prove the finiteness of the set of solutions to the equation
  \begin{equation*} y^d=1+c_1g^{m_1}+c_2g^{m_2}+c_3g^{m_3}, \quad 0<m_1<m_2<m_3, \end{equation*} where $c_i$ are $S$-units for a suitable set $S$. The proof combines consequences of the Subspace Theorem concerning integer values of analytic series at $S$-unit points with Roth's theorem for number fields. As a second result, we extend the work of Bennett and Bugeaud \cite{bennettbugead2013perfect} by proving that, for every fixed integer base $g\geq 2$, there exists a constant $q_0(g)$ such that, for every prime $q>q_0(g)$, no perfect $q$-th power admits a representation with five non-zero digits in base $g$. The proof relies on explicit lower bounds for linear forms in both Archimedean and non-Archimedean logarithms.

</details>


### [106] [Primitive Fourier Coefficients of Hermitian Klingen-Eisenstein Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08370)
*Nobuki Takeda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文确定了主同余层上由 U_{r,r} 尖形式诱导的 U_{n,n} 上 Hermitian Klingen-Eisenstein 级数的本原 Fourier 系数，并用回拉公式将其表示为 Siegel Eisenstein 级数相应系数与一显式构造的全纯自守形式的 Petersson 内积之积；在若干假设下证明了这些归一化本原 Fourier 系数的 p-整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermitian Klingen-Eisenstein 级数的 Fourier 系数是其算术性质的核心，而本原 Fourier 系数与 Petersson 内积、L-函数密切相关。作者希望把经典 Siegel 模形式中回拉公式与 Fourier 系数计算的结果推广到 Hermitian 情形，并在主同余层上给出显式表达，进而研究其 p-整性。

Method: 在 CM 扩张 E/F 上考虑 1≤r<n，取主同余层，研究由 U_{r,r} 上尖形式诱导到 U_{n,n} 的 Hermitian Klingen-Eisenstein 级数。利用 Hermitian Eisenstein 级数的回拉公式（pullback formula），将归一化本原 Fourier 系数化为相应 Siegel Eisenstein 级数的本原 Fourier 系数与 Petersson 内积的乘积。该 Petersson 内积中的自守形式被显式构造为 Hermitian theta 级数与 r 次 Siegel Eisenstein 级数乘积的有限和。

Result: 得到归一化本原 Fourier 系数的显式公式：它等于对应 Siegel Eisenstein 级数的本原 Fourier 系数乘以与显式构造自守形式的 Petersson 内积。该自守形式为 Hermitian theta 级数与 r 次 Siegel Eisenstein 级数之积的有限和。此外，在若干假设下证明了这些归一化本原 Fourier 系数的 p-整性。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Hermitian Klingen-Eisenstein 级数本原 Fourier 系数的计算归结为 Siegel Eisenstein 级数系数与显式自守形式的 Petersson 内积，并给出 p-整性结果，为研究 Hermitian 模形式的算术性质提供了工具。

Abstract: Let $E/F$ be a CM extension and let $1\le r<n$. We determine primitive Fourier coefficients of Hermitian Klingen-Eisenstein series on $\mathrm{U}_{n,n}$ induced from cusp forms on $\mathrm{U}_{r,r}$, at principal congruence level. Using the pullback formula for Hermitian Eisenstein series, we express a normalized primitive Fourier coefficient of a Klingen-Eisenstein series as the corresponding primitive Fourier coefficient of a Siegel Eisenstein series multiplied by a Petersson inner product with an explicitly constructed holomorphic automorphic form. The automorphic form in the Petersson inner product is a finite sum of products of Hermitian theta series and Siegel Eisenstein series of degree $r$. Under some hypotheses, we also prove the $p$-integrality of these normalized primitive Fourier coefficients.

</details>


### [107] [Algebraic Eisenstein classes for $\mathrm{GL}_n$ with applications to the Bianchi case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08375)
*Zhenghang Du*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文显式计算了Kings-Sprang构造的等变代数Eisenstein-Kronecker类的当前表示，并在Bianchi情形下给出了Eisenstein上同调类的纯代数构造，将其尖点常数项用K的部分Hecke L-值表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kings-Sprang构造了等变的代数Eisenstein-Kronecker类，但其具体的当前（current）表示尚未明确给出。此外，在Bianchi情形下，相关的局部对称空间不是代数簇，因此需要寻找纯代数的构造方式，并理解这些类的算术意义（如常数项与L-值的关系）。

Method: 设L为数域且包含CM域K，通过显式计算表示GL_{O_K}(O_L)-等变代数Eisenstein-Kronecker类的当前。在Bianchi情形（K为虚二次域，[L:K]=2）下，利用该计算给出Eisenstein上同调类的纯代数构造，并推导其尖点常数项。

Result: 得到了Eisenstein-Kronecker类的显式当前表示；在Bianchi情形下实现了Eisenstein上同调类的纯代数构造（尽管局部对称空间非代数簇）；将常数项表示为K的部分Hecke L-值。

Conclusion: 该工作为Eisenstein-Kronecker类提供了显式解析工具，并在非代数簇情形下建立了代数上同调类与Hecke L-值之间的联系。

Abstract: Let $L$ be a number field containing a CM field $K$. We compute an explicit current representing the $\mathrm{GL}_{\mathcal{O}_K}(\mathcal{O}_L)$-equivariant algebraic Eisenstein-Kronecker classes constructed by Kings-Sprang. In the Bianchi case, where $K$ is imaginary quadratic and $[L:K]=2$, this yields a purely algebraic construction of Eisenstein cohomology classes, even though the associated locally symmetric space is not an algebraic variety. We express their constant terms at the cusp in terms of partial Hecke $L$-values of $K$.

</details>


### [108] [Generating and generalizing MSTD sets through Markov processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08394)
*Frank He,Karol Daniewski,Steven J. Miller*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将经典MSTD问题推广到两状态马尔可夫链模型，证明和支配、差支配与平衡集合的概率在$n\to\infty$时均趋于严格正极限，并给出期望差的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MSTD问题中，由于加法可交换而减法不可交换，曾猜想几乎所有的均匀随机子集都是差支配的；但Martin与O'Bryant出人意料地证明存在正比例的和支配集合。作者希望超越独立Bernoulli模型，引入相邻包含决策相关的机制，从而更一般地刻画和差支配现象。

Method: 引入两状态平稳马尔可夫链$(X_i)_{i=0}^n$，转移概率$P(0,0)=p$、$P(1,1)=q$，令$A=\{i:X_i=1\}$；独立Bernoulli模型对应$p=1-q$，均匀随机子集对应$p=q=1/2$。采用MSTD文献中的fringe-middle方法，先证明中间部分的和与差以高概率被填满，再将比较归结为端点fringe；随后通过fringe manipulation分析边界贡献。此外结合组合方法推导期望差的闭式表达，并对有限$n$及不同$(p,q)$做数值估计。

Result: 证明中间和与中间差以高概率被完全填满，因此$|A+A|$与$|A-A|$的比较由端点fringe决定。进一步证明和支配、差支配与平衡集合的概率在$n\to\infty$时分别收敛到严格正的极限。给出有限$n$下这三类概率在若干$(p,q)$取值范围内的数值估计，并得到$\mathbb{E}[|A-A|-|A+A|]$当$n\to\infty$时的闭式表达。

Conclusion: 经典MSTD模型是该马尔可夫链框架在$p=1-q$时的特例；在更一般的相依包含机制下，和支配、差支配与平衡三种现象可以共存且各占严格正比例。结果深化了对MSTD现象普遍性与机制的理解，并提供期望差的精确刻画与数值参考。

Abstract: The classical More Sums Than Differences (MSTD) problem studies finite sets $A\subset\{0,1,\ldots,n\}$ for which $|A+A|>|A-A|$, where $A+A=\{a_1+a_2:a_1,a_2\in A\}$ and $A-A=\{a_1-a_2:a_1,a_2\in A\}$. As addition is commutative and subtraction is not, it was conjectured that as $n\to\infty$ almost all subsets $A$ chosen uniformly from the power set of $\{0,1,\ldots,n\}$ are difference-dominated, and it was thus a surprise when Martin and O'Bryant proved a positive percentage of sets are sum-dominant. We greatly generalize this model by introducing a Markov-chain framework, where the classical MSTD model is now just a special case. Let $(X_i)_{i=0}^n$ be a stationary two-state Markov chain on $\{0,1\}$ with transition probabilities $P(0,0)=p$ and $P(1,1)=q$, where $p,q\in(0,1)$. We include $i$ in $A$ exactly when $X_i=1$, and define $A=\{i\in\{0,\ldots,n\}:X_i=1\}$. The usual independent Bernoulli model is recovered when consecutive inclusion decisions are independent, equivalently when $p=1-q$. In particular, the uniformly random subset model corresponds to $p=q=1/2$. Using the fringe-middle method from the MSTD literature, we show that the middle sums and differences are filled with high probability, so the comparison between $|A+A|$ and $|A-A|$ is again governed by endpoint fringes. By fringe manipulation, we prove that the probabilities of sum-dominant, difference-dominant, and balanced sets tend to strictly positive limits as $n\to\infty$. We also give numerical estimates of these three probabilities for finite $n$ over a range of values of $p$ and $q$. Through combinatorial methods, we find a closed-form expression for $\mathbb{E}[|A-A|-|A+A|]$ as $n\to\infty$.

</details>


### [109] [On Sárközy's Local Extrema Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08424)
*Alexander P. Mangerel*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究乘性函数序列中"局部极大/极小"型模式的频率，对使得某类模式对数密度为0的所有正取值乘性函数给出精确分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注乘性函数$f:\mathbb{N}\to(0,\infty)$在相邻项比较中呈现的振荡模式，即$f(n)>\omega\max\{f(n-1),f(n+1)\}$与$f(n)<\frac1\omega\min\{f(n-1),f(n+1)\}$出现的频率。其动机之一是解决A. Sárközy于2001年提出的两个猜想：分类只有有限多个局部极大值或有限多个局部极小值的乘性函数$f:\mathbb{N}\to\mathbb{N}$。

Method: 证明基于加性函数的"pretentiousness"二分法。关键工具包括作者此前建立的加性函数版本的Matomäki-Radziwiłł定理，以及关于加性函数相邻差$g(n+1)-g(n)$集中性的新上界。

Result: 论文对至少一类模式仅以对数密度0出现的所有正取值乘性函数给出了精确分类；作为应用，以强形式解决了Sárközy的两个猜想，完成了相应乘性函数的分类。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了乘性函数局部极值出现频率的临界行为，并展示了加性函数pretentiousness二分法及相邻差集中性估计在数论函数振荡问题中的应用价值。

Abstract: Let $f: \mathbb{N} \rightarrow (0,\infty)$ be a multiplicative function, and let $ω> 0$. We consider the frequency with which patterns of the form $$ f(n) > ω\max\{f(n-1),f(n+1)\}, \quad f(n) < \frac{1}ω\min\{f(n-1),f(n+1)\} $$ occur in the sequence $(f(n))_n$, and give a sharp classification of all positive-valued multiplicative functions for which at least one of these patterns only occurs with logarithmic density $0$.
  As a particular application, we resolve, in a strong form, two conjectures of A. Sárközy from 2001, classifying all multiplicative functions $f: \mathbb{N} \rightarrow \mathbb{N}$ that have either finitely many local maxima, or finitely many local minima.
  The proof is based on a natural ``pretentiousness'' dichotomy for additive functions. Key tools in the analysis include an analogue of the Matomäki-Radziwiłłtheorem for additive functions, due to the author, as well as a new bound for the concentration of the gaps $g(n+1)-g(n)$ of an additive function $g$, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [110] [The least prime with a given primitive root and a variant of the larger sieve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08569)
*Daniel R. Johnston,Sunil Naik*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在广义黎曼假设下，证明了对于几乎所有的整数 g（非 -1 且非平方数），其最小原根素数 p_g 满足 p_g ≤ (log|g|)^{3.44}，例外个数为 O((log N)^{2.44})，改进了 Fan 和 Pollack 的 p_g ≤ (log|g|)^{19}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最小原根素数 p_g 的大小是数论中的经典问题。近期 Fan 和 Pollack 证明了统一界 p_g ≤ (log|g|)^{19}，作者希望在条件性假设（广义黎曼假设）下改进这一界，并得到"几乎所有" g 的更强估计。

Method: 证明 Gallagher 大筛法的一个新变体（其本身可能具有独立价值），并以此为工具，在广义黎曼假设下对 |g| ∈ [N, 2N] 的整数 g 进行分析，控制例外个数。此外还讨论了无条件成立的"几乎所有"界。

Result: 在广义黎曼假设下，对所有满足 |g| ∈ [N, 2N] 的 g，除至多 O((log N)^{2.44}) 个例外外，均有 p_g ≤ (log|g|)^{3.44}。该结果直接改进了 Fan 和 Pollack 的 p_g ≤ (log|g|)^{19}。

Conclusion: 通过新的大筛法变体，在广义黎曼假设下显著改进了最小原根素数的"几乎所有"上界；同时给出了无条件情形下的相关"几乎所有"界。

Abstract: For an integer $g$ not equal to $-1$ or a square, we study the least prime $p_g$ such that $g$ is a primitive root modulo $p_g$. We show that, assuming the Generalised Riemann Hypothesis, all such $g$ with $|g|\in[N,2N]$ have $p_g\leq(\log |g|)^{3.44}$ with at most $O((\log N)^{2.44})$ exceptions. This can be directly compared to the uniform bound $p_g\leq(\log |g|)^{19}$ proven recently by Fan and Pollack. To obtain our result, we prove a new variant of Gallagher's larger sieve, which may be of independent interest. In addition to our conditional result, we discuss other ``almost all" bounds for $p_g$ that hold unconditionally.

</details>


### [111] [New integer sequence OEIS A392714 counts Wronskians: fast evaluation via late-growing permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08636)
*Kian C. Shah,Arthemy V. Kiselev*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究交替复合的 2p 个严格 p 阶加权微分算子，其低阶项系数为通用常数 c(p) 乘以权重 w_1,…,w_N 的 Wronskian；通过将单项式权重代入把 |S_{2p}| 项求和缩减到小得多的 late-growing 置换集 Φ_p，实现了快速算法并求出 c(p) 至 p=18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替复合 p 阶加权微分算子后的算子仍为 p 阶，但其系数含一个仅依赖 p 的通用常数 c(p)。已知 c(1)=1、c(2)=2、c(3)=90，但 p≥4 时直接对 (2p)! 个置换做符号展开在计算上不可行，因此需要更高效的方法来确定 c(p) 的整数值及其渐近行为。

Method: 取单项式权重 w_j = x^{j-1}，将原本遍历全部 S_{2p} 置换的求和约化为遍历子集 Φ_p ⊆ S_{2p-1} ⊊ S_{2p} 的 late-growing 置换；将 c(p) 表示为下降阶乘乘积的带符号和，据此实现并加速算法。

Result: 算法成功给出所有整数值直至 c(18) = 4.881…×10^{462}。所得整数序列为新序列，已注册为 OEIS A392714；当 p≫1 时其（次）主导增长率为 log c(p) ≃ 2p²log p − b p² + o(p²)，其中 b ≥ 2.6744。

Conclusion: 通过单项式权重与 late-growing 置换的约化，绕开了对 (2p)! 置换直接展开的困难，获得了 c(p) 的整数值序列及其渐近增长率，其中常数 b ≥ 2.6744。

Abstract: The alternating composition of $N = 2p$ weighted differential operators $w_j(x)\cdot\partial_x^{\,p}$ of strict order $p$ on the line $\mathbb{R} \ni x$ is again an operator of order $p$; its coefficient is the universal constant $c(p)$ times the Wronskian of the weights $w_1,\ldots,w_N$. Lie brackets of vector fields fix $c(p=1)=1$; we want to find $c(p \geqslant 2)$: e.g., $c(2) = 2$ or $c(3) = 90$. Direct symbolic expansion (over $|S_{2p}| =(2p)!$ permutations) fails for $p \geqslant 4$. Taking the monomials $w_j = x^{j-1}$ reduces the summation to the much smaller set $Φ_p \subseteq S_{2p-1} \subsetneq S_{2p}$ of late-growing permutations. Expressing $c(p)$ as a signed sum of products of falling factorials, we implement and speed up the algorithm that gains all the integer values up to $c(18) = 4.881\ldots \cdot 10^{462}$. The resulting sequence is new, now registered as OEIS A392714; its (sub)leading-order growth rate is $\log c(p) \simeq 2p^2\log p -b p^2 + \overline{o}(p^2)$ for $p\gg 1$, with $b\geqslant 2.6744$.

</details>


### [112] [New algebraic points on covers of elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08753)
*Diana Mocanu,George C. Turcas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究与椭圆曲线度数n覆盖相关的"新点"集合C(L)_new的稀疏性，证明满足C(L)_new≠∅的数域其绝对判别式的计数上界为O(X^{1/2})或O(X/(log X)^α)，并在次数2-5中证明密度为0，附带X_0(N)等具体例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khawaja与Siksek近期猜测：对亏格≥2的光滑射影曲线C/Q，满足C(L)_new≠∅的度数n数域L占比为0（按绝对判别式排序），并在C为P^1的度数n覆盖时给出证据。本文旨在补充其工作，将类似结果推广到椭圆曲线的度数n覆盖ψ:C→E的情形。

Method: 对椭圆曲线E的度数n覆盖ψ:C→E，在适当假设下，利用数域计数工具（Bhargava–Shankar–Wang以及McGown–Thorne–Tucker关于含指定局部约束的数域计数）对满足C(L)_new≠∅的原始度数n数域L的绝对判别式进行估计，并给出具体覆盖的例子验证。

Result: 主结果：在适当假设下，绝对判别式≤X且满足C(L)_new≠∅的原始度数n数域L的不同绝对判别式个数为O(X^{1/2})或O(X/(log X)^α)（某α>0）。在度数2,3,4,5中，这类域在同次数所有数域中密度为0（度数4中在原始域中亦为0）。度数4、5为新贡献，借助B–S–W与M–T–T的工作计数具指定局部约束的数域。给出8条双椭圆模曲线X_0(N)，对100%二次域L有X_0(N)(L)_new=∅；并在LMFDB中指出度数3和5的模覆盖有类似结论。

Conclusion: 本文补充了Khawaja–Siksek的猜想性工作，将"新点数域稀疏"的结果从P^1覆盖推广到椭圆曲线覆盖，并在低次数及模曲线实例上验证了密度为0的现象，为相关猜想提供了更广的证据。

Abstract: For a smooth projective curve $C/\mathbb{Q}$ of genus $\geq 2$ and $L/\mathbb{Q}$ an extension, we write $C(L)_{\text{new}}=\{P\in C(L):\mathbb{Q}(P)=L\}$. Recent work of Khawaja and Siksek conjectures that this set is empty for $100\%$ of degree $n$ number fields $L$, when ordered by absolute discriminant. Moreover, they bring evidence towards this conjecture when $C$ is a degree $n$ cover of $\mathbb{P}^1$. We complement their work by proving analogous results for degree $n$ covers $ψ:C\to E$ of elliptic curves $E$.
  Our main result shows that, under suitable hypotheses, the number of distinct absolute discriminants at most $X$ of primitive degree $n$ fields $L$ with $C(L)_{\text{new}}\neq\varnothing$ is $O(X^{1/2})$ or $O(X/(\log X)^α)$, for some $α>0$. In degrees $2,3,4$ and $5$ we show that these fields have density $0$ among all fields of the same degree (in degree $4$, also among the primitive ones). The novelty is for degrees $4$ and $5$, where we use work of Bhargava--Shankar--Wang and McGown--Thorne--Tucker to count fields with specified local constraints.
  Moreover, we give concrete examples of $8$ bielliptic modular curves $X_0(N)$, for which $X_0(N)(L)_{\text{new}}=\emptyset$ for $100 \%$ of quadratic fields $L$. Lastly, we point out modular covers of degrees $3$ and $5$ in the LMFDB for which similar conclusions hold.

</details>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Generalizable Robustness Testing of DNN-Based Robotic Navigation Systems via XAI-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06862)
*Khizra Sohail,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可解释性引导的多目标进化方法，为DNN控制的机器人系统生成跨图像有效且可迁移的稀疏扰动鲁棒性测试，仿真中位成功率70.0%，且仿真与物理机器人失效存在显著正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN日益用于控制CPS，微小输入扰动即可引发系统级不安全行为；但现有方法多针对单张图像优化扰动且仅在仿真中评估，导致泛化性不足、实际有效性存疑。因此需要生成跨运行观测仍有效的鲁棒性测试，并验证其失效能否从仿真迁移到物理机器人。

Method: 提出可解释性引导的多目标进化方法：先通过视觉与行为聚类选出代表性图像，在其上生成稀疏扰动；用聚合的Integrated Gradients引导变异方向，聚焦影响性高的图像区域。在Gazebo中DNN控制的LeoRover上评估，开展消融实验，并在物理机器人上验证分层抽样的扰动子集。

Result: 中位成功率70.0%，优于无引导搜索的53.85%；中位超体积由0.65升至0.73。多图像优化将成功率由50.0%提升至57.5%，XAI引导进一步提升至70.0%。sim-to-real评估中仿真精确率0.95、召回率0.67，仿真与物理失效时间呈显著正相关（0.617）。

Conclusion: 多图像优化与可解释性引导搜索相结合可提升DNN控制机器人系统的鲁棒性测试效果；仿真能有效识别并优先排序可迁移失效，但部分真实失效无法在仿真中复现，故物理验证仍不可替代。

Abstract: **Context:** Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) increasingly control Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs), yet small input perturbations can cause unsafe system-level behavior. Existing approaches often optimize perturbations for individual images and evaluate them only in simulation, limiting their generalizability and practical validity.
  **Objectives:** This work aims to generate robustness tests that remain effective across operational observations and to evaluate whether the resulting failures transfer from simulation to a physical robot.
  **Methods:** We propose an explainability-guided multi-objective evolutionary approach that generates sparse perturbations over representative images selected through visual and behavioral clustering. Aggregated Integrated Gradients guide mutations toward influential image regions. We evaluate the approach on a DNN-controlled LeoRover in Gazebo, conduct an ablation study, and validate a stratified subset of perturbations on the physical robot.
  **Results:** The approach achieved a median success rate of 70.0%, compared with 53.85% for unguided search, and increased median hypervolume from 0.65 to 0.73. Multi-image optimization improved the success rate from 50.0% to 57.5%, while XAI guidance further increased it to 70.0%. In the sim-to-real evaluation, simulation achieved 0.95 precision and 0.67 recall, and simulated and physical failure times showed a significant positive correlation of 0.617.
  **Conclusion:** Combining multi-image optimization with explainability-guided search improves robustness testing for DNN-controlled robotic systems. Simulation effectively identifies and prioritizes transferable failures, but physical validation remains necessary because some real-world failures are not reproduced in simulation.

</details>


### [114] [RMRRT: Riemannian Barrier Metric RRT for Inequality-Aware Steering on Equality Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06863)
*Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RMRRT框架，在黎曼度量下统一等号与不等号约束，提升高维机器人采样规划效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统采样规划中，等号约束依赖投影、不等号约束依赖二元有效性检验（如碰撞检测）分别处理，导致探索效率低下。作者希望用统一的局部几何表达同时处理两类约束，并让探索主动远离不等号边界。

Method: RMRRT先在环境空间构造由不等式敏感障碍项组成的环境障碍度量，再通过与等号约束相关的G-正交投影诱导切空间度量；该切空间度量一致用于steering与最近邻选择，从而偏向远离不等号边界的探索并保持一阶等号一致性。度量由基于有符号距离的几何代理不等式实例化，以提供碰撞信息相关的切空间方向；硬可行性仍由标准有效性检验保证。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的多种受限操作任务中，RMRRT达到100%成功率，并相对代表性受限规划基线减少规划时间。消融研究显示该度量通过减少被拒样本、缩短路径长度提升探索质量。实验视频与源码已公开。

Conclusion: 将等号与不等号约束统一到单一黎曼几何度量中，可有效指导采样规划探索，在不牺牲硬可行性的前提下显著提升受限操作任务的效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a motion planning framework that unifies equality and inequality constraints within a single geometric formulation for sampling-based planning in high-dimensional robotic systems. In conventional sampling-based planners, equality constraints are typically enforced through projection, whereas inequality constraints are handled separately through binary validity checks such as collision testing, often leading to inefficient exploration. To address this limitation, we propose Riemannian Barrier Metric RRT (RMRRT), which constructs a unified local geometry for planning on equality-constrained manifolds. RMRRT first builds an ambient barrier metric from inequality-sensitive barrier terms and then induces a tangent-space metric via a (G)-orthogonal projection associated with the equality constraints. The resulting tangent-space metric is used consistently in both steering and nearest-neighbor selection, biasing exploration away from nearby inequality boundaries while preserving first-order equality consistency. In this work, the metric is instantiated from signed-distance-based geometric proxy inequalities to provide collision-informative tangent-space directions; hard feasibility is enforced separately through standard validity checks. Experimental results show that RMRRT achieves a 100% success rate across diverse constrained manipulation tasks in both simulation and real-world settings, while reducing planning time relative to representative constrained planning baselines. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the proposed metric improves exploration quality by reducing rejected samples and shortening path length. Experiment videos and source code are available at: https://rmrrt-anonymous.github.io

</details>


### [115] [Geometric Coherence via Weighted Matching for 3D Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reach-Avoid Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06882)
*Prajwal Vijay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究3D异构多智能体reach-avoid博弈中的任务分配质量问题，提出"几何蔓延"（Geometric Sprawl）失效模式，并设计一种基数优先的加权序贯匹配方法，以HJI拦截值作为次级权重，通过min-cost max-flow求解，在基准测试中显著提升拦截性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何结构化的3D reach-avoid场景中，仅基于基数（cardinality-only）的匹配存在反复出现的失效模式：虽存在多个最大基数分配，但部分分配产生空间上不连贯的配对与低效追捕轨迹。作者将此称为几何蔓延（Geometric Sprawl），并希望解决该问题以提升分配质量。

Method: 在Yan等人的逃逸空间框架基础上，提出基数优先的加权序贯匹配方法：先保证最大基数，再以Hamilton-Jacobi-Isaacs拦截值 z_I(s,j) 作为次级分配权重。每个序贯阶段使用min-cost max-flow后端求解；无权重基线使用同一求解器但去除权重项。在确定性35场景基准（7个族×5种初始化变体）上评估，包含两种机制：诊断性静止未匹配设置，以及带目标导向未匹配逃逸者的混合鞍点设置。

Result: 在诊断机制下，加权方法解决了15个几何压力测试实例中的13个（这些实例使无权重基线反复超时）；在混合机制下，平均捕获数从3.20提升至3.91（+22%），平均拦截高度从3.87提升至5.36（+40%）。同时报告了更低的路径曲折度和更低的角努力代理值，表明在该一阶仿真模型中追捕轨迹更平滑。

Conclusion: 以HJI拦截值作为次级权重的基数优先序贯匹配能有效缓解几何蔓延问题，在捕获数量、拦截高度与轨迹平滑度上均优于纯基数匹配。作者公开了仿真器与基准套件（GitHub链接）以支持3D reach-avoid博弈分配策略的可复现评估。

Abstract: We study assignment quality in 3D heterogeneous multi-agent reach-avoid games and identify a recurring failure mode of cardinality-only matching in geometrically structured scenarios, which we term \emph{Geometric Sprawl}. In these cases, multiple maximum-cardinality assignments are available, but some induce spatially incoherent pairings and inefficient pursuit trajectories. Building on the evasion-space framework of Yan et al.~\cite{yan2022}, we introduce a cardinality-first weighted sequential matching method in which the Hamilton--Jacobi--Isaacs interception value $z_I(s,j)$ is used as a secondary assignment weight. Each sequential stage is solved with a min-cost max-flow backend, while the unweighted baseline uses the same solver with the weight term removed. We evaluate both methods on a deterministic 35-scenario benchmark (7 families & 5 initialization variants) under two regimes: a diagnostic stationary-unmatched setting and a hybrid saddle-point setting with goal-directed unmatched evaders. On this benchmark, the weighted method resolves 13 of 15 geometric stress-test instances that cause repeated timeouts for the unweighted baseline in the diagnostic regime, and under the hybrid regime improves mean captures from $3.20$ to $3.91$ (22\% increase) and mean interception height from $3.87$ to $5.36$ (40\% increase). We also report lower path tortuosity and lower angular-effort proxy values, suggesting smoother pursuit trajectories in this first-order simulation model. We release the simulator and benchmark suite at \href{https://github.com/Prajwal-Vijay/geometric-coherence-weighted-matching}{github.com/Prajwal-Vijay/geometric-coherence-weighted-matching} to support reproducible evaluation of assignment strategies for 3D reach-avoid games.

</details>


### [116] [Does a Learned Corrector Beat a Simple Retreat? Evidence from a Frozen VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06921)
*Chenchao Sheng,Zhuang Jiang,Liuhaichen Yang,Ningwei Bai,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在冻结的π₀.₅策略上，跨四个RoboTwin任务评估运行时恢复：仅beat_block_hammer任务有+13.5pp的净增益，且简单回退基线可达到与学习式修正无显著差异的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署VLA策略的运行时恢复前，需先确认干预带来的成功率提升是否超越运行间随机波动，以及其复杂度是否优于简单动作。现有工作缺乏配对安慰剂（same-seed重跑）与简单回退基线的对照，难以区分"真实恢复"与基策略自身变异性造成的假性成功。

Method: 在冻结π₀.₅上，针对四个RoboTwin任务、共3,888对配对回合进行实验。每个测试种子配对"有/无修正"的rollout，并加入同种子基策略重跑作为安慰剂；采用种子聚类区间与预设比较规则评估净增益。同时对照固定时间触发+脚本化回到早期关节配置的回退基线、暂停控制、恒定动作控制，并进行两轮比较。

Result: 完整流水线在beat_block_hammer上提升+13.5pp（95%区间[+9.4,+17.7]），其余三个任务在部署权重下无可检测增益。在响应任务的失败基回合中，普通重跑成功率43.2%，修正后63.5%，说明大量名义恢复源于基策略自身波动。固定时间触发+脚本回退在两轮中均获净增益且与学习式流水线无检测到的差异，但预设等价标准未持续满足。暂停与恒定动作控制未获可比增益。

Conclusion: 该基准上，是否干预强烈依赖任务；要确立学习式修正的真实贡献，必须采用配对安慰剂与简单回退基线，否则容易将基策略随机波动误判为恢复效果。

Abstract: Before deploying runtime recovery for a frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policy, one must establish that an intervention improves success beyond ordinary run-to-run variation and that its complexity adds value over a simple action. We evaluate these questions on frozen $π_{0.5}$ across four RoboTwin tasks. For each test seed, we pair rollouts with and without correction and include a same-seed base-policy re-run as a placebo. Seed-cluster intervals and prespecified comparison rules assess net gains against stochastic outcome changes. Across 3,888 paired episodes, the full pipeline raises success on beat_allowbreak block_allowbreak hammer by $+13.5$\,pp (95\% interval $[+9.4,+17.7]$), with no detectable gain on the other three tasks at the deployed weight. Among failed base episodes on the responsive task, $43.2\%$ succeed on a plain re-run, compared with $63.5\%$ after correction; many nominal rescues therefore reflect the base policy's own variability. A fixed-time trigger and scripted return to an earlier joint configuration produce a net gain with no detected difference from the learned pipeline across two rounds, although our prespecified equivalence criterion is not met consistently. Pausing and a constant-action control do not yield comparable gains. On this benchmark, the decision to intervene depends strongly on the task, and a paired placebo plus a simple retreat baseline are needed to establish what learned correction contributes.

</details>


### [117] [SWAP: Stepwise Action Policy Routing for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06926)
*Mousumi Das,Aditeya Prajapati,Abrar Anwar,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWAP将多个VLA策略的组合形式化为离线强化学习问题，学习一个路由critic在每个决策步选择最合适的策略，支持在线动态切换，在真实DROID任务与LIBERO仿真中优于固定策略与路由基线，真实成功率最高提升33%，成功轨迹的机器人动作步数减少28.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLA骨干的机器人操作通常只使用单一VLA执行任务，而单个VLA在不同任务状态和环境下表现不佳，难以通用。

Method: 提出SWAP框架，将策略路由建模为离线强化学习问题，学习一个路由critic，依据当前观测在每个决策步选择最合适的策略；使机器人能在执行过程中在线切换到新策略，而非在整个episode内固定单一策略。

Result: 在真实世界DROID操作任务和LIBERO仿真实验中评估，SWAP优于固定策略执行和已有路由基线，真实任务成功率绝对提升最高达33%，同时成功轨迹的机器人动作步长减少28.3%。

Conclusion: 动态组合多个VLA策略可有效克服单一策略的局限，提升跨任务状态与环境的执行成功率与效率。

Abstract: Robot manipulation systems using Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model backbones typically use just one VLA for task execution. However, individual VLAs do not perform well across different task states and environments. We introduce a framework for dynamically composing multiple VLA policies during execution: StepWise Action Policy Routing (SWAP). SWAP formulates policy routing as an offline reinforcement learning problem, learning a routing critic that selects the most appropriate policy at each decision step given the current observation. SWAP enables robots to select new policies to execute online rather than committing to a single policy for the duration of an episode. We evaluate SWAP on both real-world DROID manipulation tasks and LIBERO simulation experiments.
