Method: 1) 维护有限候选动力学模型库；2) 基于短回溯窗口内的近期预测误差对模型进行排序；3) 对与最优（best-fit）模型在容差范围内的每个模型分别施加高阶CBF条件，从而在最优拟合自适应与全库鲁棒滤波之间连续插值；4) 允许动力学非线性依赖未知参数，无需切换模型或连续参数化估计器。

Result: 理论上证明：只要库中保留一个"安全代表性候选"，任何可行的滤波输入都满足真实CBF条件。四旋翼仿真（突发风向反转+未知负载）中，LBA-CBF在所有随机初始条件下均安全到达目标，与oracle相当；自适应与鲁棒基线成功率仅0-88%。控制回路内可运行多达250,000个模型。Crazyflie 2.1与F1TENTH实物实验验证了对风、负载释放及轮胎-路面摩擦变化的适应能力。

Conclusion: LBA-CBF通过在回溯窗口内对模型库排序并在容差带内施加多模型CBF约束，实现了无需切换模型或连续估计器即可应对突变工况的安全认证，仿真与实物均验证其优于自适应与鲁棒基线。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) certify commands through an assumed dynamics model, so an abrupt, unmeasured regime change can undermine the certificate exactly when safety matters most. We present Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions (LBA-CBF), which rank a finite bank of candidate dynamics by recent prediction error over a short look-back window and enforce the high-order CBF condition against every model within a tolerance of the best, spanning best-fit adaptation to full-bank robust filtering. The dynamics may depend nonlinearly on the unknown parameters, and no switching model or continuously parameterized estimator is required. We prove that any feasible filtered input satisfies the true CBF condition whenever a safety-representative candidate is retained. In quadrotor simulation with abrupt wind reversals and an unknown payload, LBA-CBF is safe and reaches the goal from all random initial conditions, matching an oracle, while adaptive and robust baselines achieve 0-88% success. Banks of up to 250,000 models run inside the control loop, and Crazyflie 2.1 and F1TENTH experiments demonstrate adaptation to wind, payload release, and varying tire-road friction. Code, videos, and project details are available at: https://lla-control.github.io

</details>


### [227] [DepthWorld: 3D World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08780)
*Jai Bardhan,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了DROID-3D标定数据集并训练DepthWorld世界模型，通过多视角RGB与深度联合预测获得一致的三维几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的世界模型仅用RGB训练，生成结果逐帧看似正确却无法构成一致的三维世界，而忠实的三维几何对策略评估、改进与规划至关重要。弥合这一差距需要两方面进展：面向操作任务的大规模三维监督，以及在不破坏预训练视频先验的前提下吸收该监督的架构。

Method: 提出标定流程：结合学习到的立体深度与联合因子图，将同一物理机器人采集的所有episode汇聚，联合求解其共享运动学参数与逐场景外参。应用于DROID数据集得到DROID-3D，提供稠密度量深度与重标定的多视角外参。基于Stable Video Diffusion训练DepthWorld，通过空间潜变量分块联合预测多视角RGB与深度，保持预训练VAE不变。

Result: 在90%的episode上外部相机重投影误差小于0.7像素。相同训练预算下，深度监督使RGB预测相比纯RGB基线提升+1.48 dB PSNR，同时输出可用于下游几何推理的准确度量深度。

Conclusion: 大规模标定三维监督与兼容预训练先验的架构设计可同时提升视频世界模型的RGB生成质量与几何一致性，为机器人提供可靠的三维世界模型。

Abstract: World models offer a data-driven alternative to traditional simulators for robotics, with applications spanning policy evaluation, improvement, and planning. All of these uses depend on faithful 3D geometry, yet current video-based world models are trained on RGB alone and produce rollouts that look correct frame-by-frame but do not compose into a consistent 3D world. Closing this gap requires progress on two fronts: large-scale 3D supervision for manipulation, and an architecture that can absorb it without disturbing strong pretrained video priors. We introduce a calibration pipeline that combines learned stereo depth with a joint factor graph, pooling all episodes collected from the same physical robot to recover its shared kinematic parameters alongside per-scene extrinsics. Applied to the DROID dataset, this yields DROID-3D, a calibrated 3D dataset providing dense metric depth and recalibrated multi-view extrinsics (achieving <0.7 px reprojection error on 90% of episodes for external cameras). We then train DepthWorld, a Stable Video Diffusion-based world model that jointly predicts multi-view RGB and depth via spatial latent tiling, leaving the pretrained Variational Autoencoder (VAE) unchanged. Depth supervision improves RGB prediction itself by +1.48 dB PSNR over an identical RGB-only baseline at equal training budget, while simultaneously yielding accurate metric depth for downstream geometric reasoning.

</details>


### [228] [PEARS: Physical-Prior-Guided Efficient Adaptation via Failure Reasoning and Diffusion Steering for Tactile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08784)
*Kun Song,Yiming Wang,Yilin Chen,Tianyi Ding,Jiaxin Tian,Tianqi Gong,Daolin Ma,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PEARS，一种物理先验引导的混合强化学习框架，通过物理引导力推理与触觉条件扩散引导实现预训练策略的高效在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在部署时遇到分布外（OOD）条件会显著性能退化，需要通过真实交互进行后训练，但基于强化学习的后训练通常需要大量环境交互，在操作任务中每次尝试缓慢、昂贵甚至具有破坏性，因此需要样本高效的在线适应方法。

Method: PEARS包含三个核心组件：（1）物理引导力推理（PFR）模块，在每个回合后利用VLM中编码的物理先验，从视觉结果与触觉交互历史中诊断失败并更新任务合适的接触力边界；（2）高频混合力-位置控制器，在接触过程中执行这些力边界；（3）触觉条件扩散引导强化学习，调整冻结的流匹配策略的潜在噪声，以纠正自由空间运动与接触时机误差，而不更新基础模型。

Result: 仿真中成功率比各任务最强基线提升12.4–37.4个百分点；达到特定成功率阈值所需交互回合数相比最快基线减少最多53.2%；真实实验中白板擦除成功率95%，移液管液体吸取成功率90%。

Conclusion: 将PFR模块与策略引导相结合，可在减少昂贵交互的同时加速预训练策略的在线适应，验证了物理先验与触觉反馈协同的有效性。

Abstract: Pretrained robotic policies can suffer substantial performance degradation under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions encountered during deployment, motivating post-training through real-world interaction. However, reinforcement-learning (RL)-based post-training typically requires substantial environment interactions, a burden that is especially significant in manipulation, where each trial can be slow, costly, or destructive. Therefore, we present PEARS, a physics-prior-guided hybrid RL framework for sample-efficient online adaptation of pretrained policies with tactile feedback. After each episode, its physics-guided force reasoning (PFR) module uses physical priors encoded in a vision-language model (VLM) to diagnose failures from the visual outcome and tactile interaction history and update task-appropriate contact-force bounds. A high-frequency hybrid force-position controller then enforces these bounds during contact. Complementarily, tactile-conditioned diffusion steering reinforcement learning adjusts the latent noise of the frozen flow-matching policy to correct errors in free-space motion and contact timing without updating the base model. In simulation, PEARS improves success rates by 12.4-37.4 percentage points over the strongest per-task baselines. PEARS also reduces the number of interaction episodes required for a certain success threshold by up to 53.2% relative to the fastest baseline. In real-world experiments, PEARS achieves success rates of 95% on Whiteboard Erasing and 90% on Pipette Liquid Aspiration. These results show that combining the PFR module with policy steering can accelerate adaptation while reducing costly interactions. The project website is available at https://song-kun.github.io/pears.

</details>


### [229] [QF3: Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08789)
*Chung Min Kim,Brent Yi,David McAllister,Hongsuk Choi,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Jinkun Cao,Ken Goldberg,Pieter Abbeel,Carmelo Sferrazza,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QF3是一种在线离策略强化学习算法，通过流匹配与经滤波的critic动作梯度联合训练流策略，实现从零训练并零样本迁移到硬件的人形运动策略，速度快于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略已成为从演示学习机器人行为的标准方法，但强化学习对于改进预训练流策略或通过交互从零学习仍至关重要。现有流RL方法（如FPO++）多为在线策略，训练效率低，亟需高效的离策略流RL算法。

Method: QF3采用流匹配加critic动作梯度，梯度通过流输出的一步预测进行反向传播；为使更新保持在critic与该预测可靠的区域，仅将critic梯度应用于保持接近回放动作的动作维度；并配以高吞吐离策略训练配方。

Result: QF3是首个从零训练人形运动策略并零样本迁移到硬件的离策略流RL方法；训练人形运动和运动跟踪策略相比近期在线策略流RL方法FPO++获得10倍墙钟加速；在ABC-Sim和Robomimic任务上微调预训练流式操作策略亦有效。

Conclusion: QF3既能从零学习机器人策略，也能精炼从演示中获得的策略，证明了离策略流RL在人形运动与操作任务上的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Flow policies have become a standard policy class for learning robot behaviors from demonstrations, but reinforcement learning is still critical for improving pre-trained flow policies or learning them from scratch through interaction. We introduce QF3 (Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients), an online off-policy RL algorithm that trains a flow policy with flow matching plus the critic's action gradient, backpropagated through a one-step prediction of the flow's output. To keep updates where the critic and this prediction are reliable, QF3 applies the critic gradient only to action dimensions that stay near the replay action. To our knowledge, QF3 is the first off-policy flow RL method to train humanoid locomotion policies from scratch and transfer them zero-shot to hardware. Paired with a high-throughput off-policy training recipe, it trains humanoid locomotion and motion-tracking policies with a 10x wall-clock speedup over FPO++, a recent on-policy flow RL method. We further apply QF3 to fine-tune pretrained flow-based manipulation policies on both ABC-Sim and Robomimic tasks. These results suggest that QF3 can both learn robot policies from scratch and refine those acquired from demonstrations. Website: https://qf3-rl.github.io/

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [An example of the non-existence of coequalizers in the category of the A$_{\infty}$-categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07017)
*Mattia Ornaghi*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 证明了严格含单位（或非含单位）A∞-代数与A∞-范畴的相应范畴不存在余等化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在研究A∞-代数与A∞-范畴这类高阶代数结构的范畴是否具有余极限性质，具体考察余等化子这类基本余极限是否存在。

Method: 通过对严格含单位（或非含单位）A∞-态射与A∞-函子的范畴构造进行反例论证或一般性矛盾推导。

Result: 证明了严格含单位（或非含单位）A∞-代数范畴以及对应的A∞-范畴，在取严格含单位（或非含单位）A∞-态射与A∞-函子的情况下，均不存在余等化子。

Conclusion: A∞-代数与A∞-范畴的上述范畴不具备余完备性中的余等化子结构，说明其范畴论性质受到显著限制。

Abstract: We prove that the categories of strictly unital (resp.\ non unital) A$_{\infty}$-algebras and A$_{\infty}$-categories, with strictly unital (resp.\ non unital) A$_{\infty}$-morphisms and A$_{\infty}$-functors have no coequalizers.

</details>


### [231] [A Finite-Geometric Obstruction and a Polytopal Separation of Buchstaber Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07631)
*Suyoung Choi,Hyeontae Jang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 构造了一个多面体单纯球面，其模2特征映射存在但模3特征映射不存在，从而不存在整特征映射；证明多面体球面的Buchstaber数可严格小于实Buchstaber数，并否定环面提升问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Buchstaber数（实Buchstaber数）与多面体单纯球面特征映射存在性之间的关系，并考察环面提升问题是否成立。

Method: 构造一个具体的多面体单纯球面，分析其模2、模3以及整特征映射的存在性。

Result: 该球面存在模2特征映射，但不存在模3特征映射，因此也不存在整特征映射；由此得到Buchstaber数严格小于实Buchstaber数的例子。

Conclusion: 否定了环面提升问题，表明多面体球面的Buchstaber数与实Buchstaber数可以严格不同。

Abstract: We construct a polytopal simplicial sphere that admits a mod 2 characteristic map but no mod 3 characteristic map, and hence no integral one. This shows that the Buchstaber number of a polytopal sphere can be strictly smaller than its real Buchstaber number, and gives a negative answer to the toric lifting problem.

</details>


### [232] [Semifree Cochain Models for Fibrations Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07769)
*Yanning Guo,Ruizhi Huang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文构造了路径连通底空间上Serre纤维化的上链模型，系数取自任意域。该构造精化了Brown的扭曲张量积，得到底空间奇异上链代数上的微分分次模模型；在纤维同调逐阶有限维且基本群作用逐阶幂零时，该模型是由纤维上同调生成的半自由消解，推广了单连通底空间的经典构造。应用方面，证明任意半自由消解在基点到基点的基变换后都能恢复纤维上同调，并给出相关谱序列坍塌页数的显式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Serre纤维化的上链模型（如Brown扭曲张量积）在底空间单连通时有良好性质，但对非单连通底空间缺乏由纤维上同调生成半自由消解的系统构造，因此需要精化模型以推广到一般路径连通底空间。

Method: 精化Brown的扭曲张量积，构造底空间奇异上链代数上的微分分次模模型；在纤维同调逐阶有限维且基本群作用逐阶幂零的条件下，验证其为以纤维上同调为生成元的半自由消解。

Result: 所构造模型是底空间上链代数上的半自由消解；任意半自由消解经基变换到基点后可恢复纤维上同调；得到关联谱序列坍塌页的显式上界。

Conclusion: 该工作将单连通底空间的经典上链模型推广至路径连通底空间，统一了半自由消解与纤维上同调之间的关系，并为谱序列坍塌页提供了可计算界。

Abstract: We construct cochain models for Serre fibrations over path-connected bases with coefficients in an arbitrary field. Our construction refines Brown's twisted tensor product to produce a differential graded module model over the singular cochain algebra of the base. If the homology of the fibre is finite dimensional in each degree and on which the action of the fundamental group of the base is degreewise nilpotent, then this model is a semifree resolution generated by the cohomology of the fibre. This extends the classical construction for simply connected bases. As applications, we compare the resulting model with arbitrary semifree resolutions, showing that every such resolution recovers the cohomology of the fibre after base change to the basepoint, and obtain an explicit bound on the page at which an associated spectral sequence collapses.

</details>


### [233] [On open covers of aspherical spaces satisfying $π_1$-constraints and bounded Adamson cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07932)
*Arturo Espinosa Baro,Stephan Mescher*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 研究非球面空间的 F-范畴，借助有界 Adamson 上同调给出上同调下界、极大性结果及有界上同调类，应用于流形间一次映射下 F-范畴的单调性，并给出对 Capovilla–Löh–Moraschini 问题的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F-范畴刻画空间被基本群属于给定子群族 F 的开集覆盖的最小规模，是有界上同调与覆盖理论交叉的重要不变量。本文旨在为一般非球面空间的 F-范畴建立上同调型下界与极大性判据，并厘清有界上同调类的普适性与消没性质，以解决该不变量在映射下的单调性及相关公开问题。

Method: 引入并研究子群族的有界 Adamson 上同调，讨论有界上同调类的普适性（universality）；据此构造有界上同调类并利用其消没性质作为判定 F-范畴的上同调下界工具，进而证明极大性结果。

Result: 对非球面空间的 F-范畴得到：上同调下界估计、一个极大性定理，以及关键的具特定消没性质的有界上同调类。在应用上，给出流形间一次映射（degree-one maps）下 F-范畴单调性方面的若干结果，并构造反例否定 Capovilla–Löh–Moraschini 提出的一个问题。

Conclusion: 有界 Adamson 上同调及其普适性为研究 F-范畴提供了有效框架，所得下界与极大性结果统一并推广了已有结论，同时通过映射单调性与反例澄清了该不变量的边界行为。

Abstract: Given a topological space $X$ and a family of subgroups $\mathcal{F}$ of $π_1(X)$, the $\mathcal{F}$-category of $X$ is given as one less than the cardinality of the smallest cover of $X$ by open subsets whose fundamental groups lie in $\mathcal{F}$. In this article we study $\mathcal{F}$-categories of aspherical spaces and obtain cohomological lower bounds, a maximality result and bounded cohomology classes whose vanishing properties are crucial for determining $\mathcal{F}$-categories. For this purpose, we study the bounded Adamson cohomology of a family of subgroups and discuss universality properties of bounded cohomology classes. We apply our techniques to derive some applications to the study of the monotonicity of $\mathcal{F}$-categories under degree-one maps between manifolds and to provide a counterexample to a question of Capovilla, Löh and Moraschini.

</details>


### [234] [A cubical approach to homology theories for hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08196)
*Syed Hadi Ali Zaidi,Samira Sahar Jamil*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文引入超图的三种同调理论（Γ-同调、□-同调、×-同调），证明它们两两不同构且区别于嵌入同调；并引入超图同伦概念，证明□-同调在同伦下不变，而Γ-同调与×-同调不满足同伦不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为超图建立系统化的同调理论与同伦理论，厘清不同构造所得同调之间的关系，并借助同伦不变性与切除性质刻画各理论的差异。

Method: 构造三种超图同调（Γ-同调、□-同调、×-同调）；引入推广图离散同伦的超图同伦概念；利用同伦不变性检验与切除（excision）性质进行比较分析。

Result: 三种同调两两不同构，且均不同于超图的嵌入同调；□-同调在同伦下不变，Γ-同调与×-同调不具备同伦不变性；基于切除性质发现Γ-同调的独特结构行为，可进一步将其与□-同调区分。

Conclusion: 该工作为超图提供了多种本质不同的同调理论及相应的同伦框架，表明□-同调在同伦意义下更为稳健，而Γ-同调展现出独特的结构性特征，从而深化了对超图拓扑不变量的理解。

Abstract: We introduce three homology theories for hypergraphs, namely $Γ$-homology, $\Box$-homology, and $\times$-homology, and show that they are pairwise non-isomorphic and distinct from the embedded homology of hypergraphs. We further introduce a notion of homotopy for hypergraphs that extends the discrete homotopy theory of graphs. Among the homology theories considered, we prove that $\Box$-homology is invariant under this homotopy, whereas $Γ$-homology and $\times$-homology fail to satisfy homotopy invariance. Based on excision, we also identify a distinctive structural behavior exhibited by $Γ$-homology that further differentiates it from $\Box$-homology.

</details>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Correlated Low-Energy Model of Monolayer 1H-NbS$_2$: A cRPA+DMFT Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06912)
*Karollaine C. Leite,Jorge L. B. de Faria,Luiz Antonio Ribeiro Junior,Alexandre C. Dias,Helena Bragança,Alberto S. de Arruda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合第一性原理Wannier插值、cRPA和DFT+DMFT，构建了单层1H-NbS₂的有效的单带模型，cRPA给出的重整化局域相互作用U=1.138 eV即可稳定强关联金属态，重现ARPES和STS谱中的低能特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层1H-NbS₂具有孤立的、以Nb-d_{z²}为主导的金属能带，是研究单带电子关联的理想平台。但以往处理采用多轨道cRPA得到的较大U(约1.8 eV)、最近邻库仑项V及电声耦合等复杂机制，是否存在一个更简约的单带描述尚不明确。

Method: 结合第一性原理Wannier插值、约束随机相位近似(cRPA)与DFT+DMFT计算。在孤立的Nb-d_{z²}带内直接定义cRPA目标子空间以获得重整化局域相互作用，并构造有效单带模型进行DMFT求解，与静态DFT+U作对比。

Result: 有效单带模型准确再现低能电子结构，复现ARPES和STS dI/dV谱中的特征(包括Van Hove奇异性)。静态DFT+U无法描述关联金属性，而DFT+DMFT成功刻画谱权重的重新分布与动态关联。cRPA给出U_cRPA=1.138 eV，稳定强关联金属态且在降温下保持稳健。该单带模型得到的谱展宽与更复杂处理(更大U约1.8 eV、V项、电声耦合)所报道的结果相近。

Conclusion: 一个一致下折叠的局域相互作用单独就足以捕捉单层1H-NbS₂实验观测低能行为的关键方面，表明该体系可由最小单带模型有效描述，无需引入额外复杂机制。

Abstract: Monolayer $1\mathrm{H}\text{-}\mathrm{NbS}_2$ hosts a well-separated metallic band of predominant Nb-$d_{z^2}$ character, providing an ideal platform to probe electronic correlations within a single-band setting. In this work, we present a comprehensive study of $1\mathrm{H}\text{-}\mathrm{NbS}_2$ by combining first-principles Wannier interpolation, constrained random-phase approximation (cRPA), and DFT+DMFT. We show that an effective single-band model accurately captures the low-energy electronic structure, reproducing key experimental features from ARPES and STS $dI/dV$ spectra, including a characteristic Van Hove singularity. While static DFT+$U$ fails to describe the correlated metallic nature of the system, DFT+DMFT successfully accounts for the spectral weight redistribution and dynamic correlations. By defining the cRPA target subspace directly within the isolated Nb-$d_{z^2}$ band, we obtain a renormalized local interaction of $U_{\mathrm{cRPA}} = 1.138~\mathrm{eV}$. This interaction stabilizes a strongly correlated metallic state that remains robust upon cooling. Remarkably, this minimal single-band model captures a spectral broadening similar to that previously reported in more elaborate treatments - incorporating larger local interactions ($U \sim 1.8~\mathrm{eV}$, derived from a multiorbital cRPA construction), intersite Coulomb terms ($V$), and electron-phonon coupling - demonstrating that a consistently downfolded local interaction alone can already capture key aspects of the experimentally observed low-energy behavior of monolayer $1\mathrm{H}\text{-}\mathrm{NbS}_2$.

</details>


### [236] [Disorder into Correlation: A Microscopic Mechanism for Sub-McIntyre Avalanche Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06915)
*Shafat Shahnewaz,Hannaneh Karimi,Joe C. Campbell,Avik W. Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出，雪崩倍增本质随机，但实验观测到低于McIntyre局域场理论最小值的过剩噪声。作者提出极性光学声子发射（限制于狭窄前向锥）与弹性合金散射（使场准直热载流子扩散出锥）之间的竞争，解释噪声抑制的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现的过剩噪声低于McIntyre局域场理论预言的极小值，其微观起源尚未被充分理解，需要新的机制解释这种噪声抑制现象。

Method: 从微观散射机制出发，分析极性光学声子发射（限制在狭窄前向锥内）与弹性合金散射之间的竞争；合金散射将场准直的热载流子扩散出该锥，从而抑制声子冷却。

Result: 声子冷却受抑制使电离路径收窄至死区之外，并将连续电离同步为动态阶梯结构。该机制解释了Sb基合金与HgCdTe的低噪声特性。

Conclusion: 合金无序可作为器件噪声的设计参数，为低噪声雪崩倍增材料的设计提供新思路。

Abstract: Avalanche multiplication is intrinsically stochastic, yet experiments reveal excess noise below the minimum predicted by McIntyre's local-field theory. The microscopic origin of this noise suppression remains incompletely understood. We show that polar-optical-phonon emission, confined to a narrow forward cone, competes with elastic alloy scattering that diffuses field-collimated hot carriers out of the cone. Suppressed phonon cooling narrows the ionization path beyond the dead space and synchronizes successive ionizations into a dynamic staircase. This explains the low noise of Sb-based alloys and HgCdTe and makes alloy disorder a design parameter.

</details>


### [237] [Heavy-fermion hybridization waves from Dirac spinons near a Kondo-breakdown quantum phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07146)
*Pedro M. Cônsoli,Matthias Vojta,Onur Erten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出在无磁序的阻挫Kondo晶格中，重费米液体在接近Kondo屏蔽崩溃的量子相变时可自然形成杂化波，其机制源于局域磁矩形成Dirac自旋液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在多种顺磁重费米子材料中观察到空间调制屏蔽现象，需要理论机制解释此类杂化波在无磁序阻挫Kondo晶格中的产生。

Method: 采用parton平均场理论与微扰计算，研究最小Kondo-Heisenberg晶格模型。

Result: 重费米液体在接近Kondo屏蔽崩溃、局域磁矩形成Dirac自旋液体的量子相变时可自然产生杂化波；其强度与电子和自旋子能带的运动学条件相关，杂化波的空间周期由连接Dirac自旋子色散节点与电子能带低能部分的动量Q决定。

Conclusion: 该机制为无磁序阻挫Kondo晶格中杂化波的出现提供了理论解释，并揭示了其周期性与能带几何之间的定量联系。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation of spatially modulated screening in different paramagnetic heavy-fermion materials, we propose a theoretical mechanism for the emergence of such hybridization waves in frustrated Kondo lattices with no magnetic order. By applying a parton mean-field theory and a perturbative calculation to a minimal Kondo-Heisenberg lattice model, we demonstrate that a heavy Fermi liquid can naturally develop hybridization waves when it approaches a quantum phase transition where Kondo screening breaks down and the local magnetic moments form a Dirac spin liquid. We relate the strength of this mechanism to a kinematic condition on the electronic and spinon band structures and show that the spatial periodicity of the hybridization wave is set by a momentum $\vec{Q}$ connecting the nodes in the dispersion of the Dirac spinons to low-energy portions of the electronic band structure.

</details>


### [238] [Chiral Parafermions in Floquet $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Topological Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07156)
*Melissa Will,Harald Schmid,Jan Egger,Adam Gammon-Smith,Michael Knap,Frank Pollmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在广义Kitaev蜂巢模型（clock自由度）中通过三步周期驱动实现了$\mathbb{Z}_N$ Floquet拓扑序，揭示了手性parafermionic Floquet边缘模式、分数量子信息输运、任意子动力学嬗变，并证明在Clifford甜点可用stabilizer模拟、远离甜点短时MPS仍保持手性边缘响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet拓扑序将拓扑拓展到非平衡领域，可产生平衡态不存在的现象（如任意子的动力学嬗变）；本文旨在实现并刻画$\mathbb{Z}_N$ Floquet拓扑序，将其作为探索本质非平衡拓扑现象的平台。

Method: 采用三步周期驱动构造广义Kitaev蜂巢模型（含clock自由度）；用受约束parafermion耦合守恒$\mathbb{Z}_N$规范场的涌现描述分析系统；用关联函数分辨边缘模式传播，用radical chiral unitary index刻画分数量子信息输运并探测交换统计；在体态用非平衡loop序参量研究动力学任意子嬗变；在Clifford甜点用stabilizer模拟，偏离该点用短时矩阵乘积态（MPS）模拟。

Result: 驱动系统具有约束parafermion与守恒$\mathbb{Z}_N$规范场的涌现描述；边界处产生手性parafermionic Floquet边缘模式，其传播可由关联函数分辨，其分数量子信息输运由radical chiral unitary index刻画，并可用其动力学探测交换统计；在Floquet甜点（驱动变为Clifford）这些现象可用stabilizer模拟访问；远离该点的短时MPS模拟表明手性边缘响应在可及时间内持续存在。

Conclusion: $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Kitaev模型可作为在qudit量子处理器上探索本质非平衡拓扑现象的理想平台。

Abstract: Floquet topological order (FTO) extends topology beyond equilibrium, giving rise to phenomena, such as the dynamical transmutation of anyons. Here, we realize $\mathbb{Z}_N$ FTO in a generalized Kitaev honeycomb model with clock degrees of freedom using a three-step periodic drive. The driven system admits an emergent description in terms of constrained parafermions coupled to conserved $\mathbb{Z}_N$ gauge fields. At the boundary, the drive generates chiral parafermionic Floquet edge modes. We resolve their propagation through correlation functions, characterize their fractional quantum-information transport through the radical chiral unitary index, and use their dynamics to probe exchange statistics. In the bulk, we explore the dynamical anyon transmutation with a non-equilibrium loop order parameter. At a Floquet sweet spot, where the drive becomes Clifford, these phenomena can be accessed using stabilizer simulations. Short-time matrix-product-state simulations away from this point show that the chiral edge response persists at accessible times. Our results establish $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Kitaev models as a setting for exploring intrinsically non-equilibrium topological phenomena on qudit-based quantum processors.

</details>


### [239] [Local-moment magnets on all close-packed lattices are equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07158)
*Sam Studdy,Alwyn Jose Raja,R. Ganesh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文揭示了所有密堆积晶格上经典磁体之间的隐藏对称性，并构建了统一的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fcc格子上受挫的Heisenberg反铁磁体是典型例子，但fcc只是众多密堆积结构之一。作者希望找到联系所有这些具有相同受挫局部几何结构的密堆积晶格上磁体的统一规律。

Method: 作者证明了一种源自紧束缚模型类似性质的隐藏对称性，在仅考虑最近邻($J_1$)和次近邻($J_2$)耦合时成立，保证所有密堆积晶格在Luttinger-Tisza层面等价。基于此构建了Heisenberg、XY和Ising磁体在任意密堆积晶格上关于$J_1$和$J_2$的统一相图。

Result: 该对称性保证所有密堆积晶格基态能量相同，投影到堆垛平面上时基态动量一致。在统一相图中，每个相的基态简并度在所有密堆积晶格上相同，即使考虑多Q螺旋也是如此。该相图可方便地推广到由堆垛三角层组成的任何磁体。

Conclusion: 经典基态之上的涨落不受任何对称性约束，这表明不同密堆积结构可能在order-by-disorder选择中表现出有趣差异。

Abstract: The Heisenberg antiferromagnet on the fcc lattice is a canonical example of frustration. The fcc lattice is but one of an infinite number of close-packed structures, all of which have the same frustration-inducing local geometry. In this letter, we demonstrate a hidden symmetry that relates classical magnets on all close-packed lattices. This symmetry derives from a similar property in tight binding models that may explain the disordered low-temperature structures of lithium and sodium metals. In magnets, the symmetry holds when couplings are restricted to nearest ($J_1$) and next-nearest neighbours ($J_2$). It guarantees that all close-packed lattices are equivalent at the Luttinger-Tisza level. For any given values of $J_1$ and $J_2$, they have the same ground-state energy. Their ground state momenta are identical when projected onto the stacking plane. We construct a unified phase diagram as a function of $J_1$ and $J_2$ couplings for Heisenberg, XY and Ising magnets on any close-packed lattice. In each phase, the ground-state degeneracy is the same on all close-packed lattices, even after accounting for multi-Q spirals. This phase diagram can be easily adapted to any magnet composed of stacked triangular layers with similar couplings. Fluctuations above the classical ground state(s) are not related by any symmetry. This suggests that close-packed structures can show interesting differences in order-by-disorder selection.

</details>


### [240] [Odd-parity magnon spin polarization from chiral four-spin exchange in a honeycomb antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07291)
*Bektur Murzaliev,Mikhail Titov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 手性四自旋交换在零磁场下于补偿蜂窝反铁磁体中产生动量依赖的磁振子自旋；在Néel矢量偏离层法线θ0时，该相互作用以∝cosθ0sin²θ0的幅度对亚晶格间磁振子配对产生贡献，双轴各向异性混合纵向自旋扇区，生成动量奇的非量子化模式自旋，而亚晶格交换关系保持磁振子能量互易；温度梯度通过重新分布磁振子占据数沿Néel轴诱导自旋密度，反转四自旋耦合会反转模式自旋与自旋密度但不变磁振子能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零磁场下补偿反铁磁体中实现动量依赖的磁振子自旋（自旋-动量锁定/非互易自旋输运）通常需要磁场或Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用；本文旨在利用手性四自旋交换这一内禀相互作用在无磁场条件下产生此类效应，并借助MnPS3的磁性几何提供物理动机。

Method: 基于补偿蜂窝反铁磁体的磁振子理论（线性自旋波/玻色子化）：设定Néel矢量相对层法线倾斜θ0；引入手性四自旋交换，其以∝cosθ0sin²θ0的幅度贡献亚晶格间磁振子配对；叠加双轴各向异性以混合两个纵向自旋扇区；推导磁振子能量与模式自旋的对称性关系（亚晶格交换导致的能量互易性）；用温度梯度驱动的磁振子占据再分布计算自旋密度。

Result: 四自旋交换在最低（谐波）阶即产生亚晶格间磁振子配对；双轴各向异性混合纵向自旋扇区后出现非量子化的、动量为奇的模式自旋，而亚晶格交换关系保证磁振子能量保持互易（无能量非互易）；温度梯度由此沿Néel轴产生自旋密度；反转四自旋耦合符号会同时反转模式自旋与该自旋密度，但磁振子能量不变。MnPS3的磁性几何符合该模型设定，但实现该相互作用需要与模型对称性假设相容的结构修改。

Conclusion: 手性四自旋交换可在零磁场下于补偿蜂窝反铁磁体中驱动动量依赖的磁振子自旋与热诱导自旋密度，提供了一种无需磁场或DM相互作用的自旋-动量锁定机制；其可逆性（反转耦合即反转自旋响应而不改变能谱）使其具备可调控性；实验实现需针对MnPS3类材料进行满足对称性要求的结构修饰。

Abstract: Chiral four-spin exchange generates a momentum-dependent magnon spin in a compensated honeycomb antiferromagnet at zero magnetic field. For a Néel vector tilted by an angle $θ_0$ from the layer normal, the interaction contributes to intersublattice magnon pairing already at harmonic order, with an amplitude proportional to $\cosθ_0\sin^2θ_0$. Biaxial anisotropy mixes the two longitudinal-spin sectors, producing nonquantized mode spins that are odd in momentum, while a sublattice-exchange relation keeps the magnon energies reciprocal. A temperature gradient then induces a spin density along the Néel axis through a redistribution of magnon occupations. Reversing the four-spin coupling reverses this spin density and the mode spins without changing the magnon energies. MnPS$_3$ motivates the magnetic geometry; realizing the interaction requires a structural modification compatible with the symmetry assumptions of the model.

</details>


### [241] [Two-exciton bound states in the Merrifield exciton model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07915)
*Ning Wu,Runtian Li,Dazhi Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构造精确双激子Bloch态并采用平面波拟设处理非均匀三对角矩阵，完整求解了有限尺寸Merrifield链中的双激子问题，解析地给出了双激子束缚态的参数区域、类型及演化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双激子束缚态的形成与动力学对利用多激子现象和设计新一代有机半导体材料至关重要。尽管分子间激子-激子相互作用或多能级分子内构型等机制可产生束缚态，但其底层晶格动力学仍缺乏基本理解。Merrifield激子模型刻画了单格点双激发态与双粒子散射连续谱之间的量子混合，是研究该问题的理想框架。

Method: 在有限尺寸Merrifield链中构造一组精确的双激子Bloch态，并采用平面波拟设处理由此产生的非均匀三对角矩阵，从而对双激子问题进行严格求解。

Result: 解析地给出了支持双激子束缚态的参数区域，证明最多只能出现两类不同的束缚态，分别位于散射连续谱之上或之下；并定量解释了这两类束缚态在整个布里渊区内随总波数的演化行为。得到了本征能量和波函数的闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为双激子问题提供了完整且严格的解，所得闭式本征能量与波函数可作为探索相关分子聚集体中激子动力学与光学响应的有价值的基准。

Abstract: Understanding the formation and dynamics of two-exciton bound states (BSs) is crucial for harnessing multi-exciton phenomena and designing next-generation organic semiconductor materials. While various mechanisms such as intermolecular exciton-exciton interactions or multi-level intramolecular configurations can generate these BSs, a fundamental understanding of their underlying lattice dynamics remains a key challenge. Within this context, the Merrifield exciton model stands out as a premier framework for capturing the intricate quantum mixing between single-site doubly-excited states and two-particle scattering continuum. In this work, by constructing a set of exact two-exciton Bloch states and applying a plane-wave ansatz to treat the resulting inhomogeneous tridiagonal matrices, we provide a complete, rigorous solution to the two-exciton problem in a finite-size Merrifield chain. We analytically map the parameter regions that support two-exciton BSs, proving that at most two distinct types of BSs can emerge, located either above or below the scattering continuum. Furthermore, we provide a quantitative interpretation of how these two types of BSs evolve as a function of the total wave number within the whole Brillouin zone. The obtained closed-form expressions for the eigenenergies and wave functions offer valuable benchmarks for exploring exciton dynamics and optical responses in related molecular aggregates.

</details>


### [242] [Gradual Localization of Itinerant Cobalt 3d Electrons in the Pyrochlore Ferromagnetic Metal LuInCo4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08266)
*Taiki Shiotani,Subaru Yoshimoto,Yoshikazu Tabata,Yusuke Nambu,Katsuki Kinjo,Kazuhiro Nawa,Taku J. Sato,Takeshi Waki,Hiroyuki Nakamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过粉末与单晶中子衍射研究了Co烧绿石晶格巡游铁磁体LuInCo4的微观磁态，确认其为零场下简单共线铁磁结构，并观测到随降温展宽的q=0漫散射，据此提出低温变磁转变源于巡游Co-3d电子逐步局域化增强的铁磁不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LuInCo4具有钴烧绿石晶格，是研究电子局域化与巡游铁磁性相互作用的候选体系，但其微观磁基态（是否存在反铁磁或螺旋调制）及低温变磁转变的微观机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用粉末和单晶中子衍射测量，分析零场磁衍射谱；通过单晶实验检验反铁磁序与长周期螺旋磁调制，并观测q=0附近的磁漫散射及其倒空间强度分布随温度的变化。

Result: 零场粉末中子衍射谱可由简单q=0共线铁磁结构很好描述，有序Co磁矩约0.9 μB；单晶中子衍射表明既无长程反铁磁序，也无长周期螺旋磁调制；同时在q=0附近观测到磁漫散射，且其倒空间强度分布随降温逐渐展宽。

Conclusion: 结果表明LuInCo4是研究钴基金属烧绿石化合物中电子局域化与巡游电子铁磁性相互作用的模型体系；其低温变磁转变可用巡游Co-3d电子逐步局域化所增强的铁磁不稳定性来解释。

Abstract: We investigated the microscopic magnetic state of the itinerant-electron ferromagnet LuInCo4 with a cobalt pyrochlore lattice by means of powder and single-crystal neutron diffraction measurements. The powder neutron diffraction profiles at zero field are well described by a simple q = 0 collinear ferromagnetic structure with an ordered Co moment of 0.9 uB. Single-crystal neutron diffraction experiments further suggest the absence of both antiferromagnetic order and long-period helical magnetic modulation. In addition, diffuse magnetic scattering was observed around q = 0, with its intensity distribution gradually broadened in reciprocal space upon cooling. Based on these observations, we propose a possible microscopic origin of the low-temperature metamagnetic transition in terms of a ferromagnetic instability enhanced by gradual localization of the itinerant Co-3d electrons. These results suggest that LuInCo4 provides a model system for investigating the interplay between electron localization and itinerant-electron ferromagnetism in cobalt-based metallic pyrochlore compounds.

</details>


### [243] [Symmetry protected quantum many body scars through half-gauging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08304)
*Weslei B. Fontana,Fabrizio G. Oliviero,Yi-Ping Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究对称性缺陷插入与量子多体疤痕现象的相互作用，利用嵌入机制构造的随机矩阵形式哈密顿量模型，分析缺陷插入后疤痕态存在的条件，并对半规范变换进行详细分析，揭示了由底层对称性保护的疤痕态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体疤痕态因其对热化行为的违反而备受关注，但其在对称性缺陷和规范变换等操作下的稳定性与调控机制尚不清楚。本文旨在探索对称缺陷插入与量子多体疤痕之间的关联，理解疤痕态在对称性操作下的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用嵌入机制定义随机矩阵形式（stochastic matrix form）的哈密顿量，构造出具有零能多体疤痕态的模型类。在此基础上分析缺陷插入条件，并对链系统中的半规范变换（half-gauging）进行详细分析，该操作在系统中引入非平凡界面。

Result: 给出了缺陷插入后仍保持疤痕态的条件；通过对半规范链的详细分析，发现半规范变换引入非平凡界面，产生了由底层对称性保护的疤痕态。

Conclusion: 半规范疤痕系统的结论具有一般性，可为高维疤痕物理及其鲁棒性研究提供有价值的方向。

Abstract: We study the interplay between symmetry defects insertion and the phenomena of quantum many body scarring. We exploit a class of models obtained from an embedding mechanism that defines a stochastic matrix form hamiltonian, hosting zero energy many body scarred states. Such models display an interesting playground to explore the consequences of dualities, half-gauging operations and so on. We first show that inside this class of models, what are the conditions to still have scared states after defect insertion. The main result of our work is a detailed analysis of half-gauging in such chains, which introduces a non-trivial interface in our system, leading to scarred states protected by the underlying symmetries. The conclusions for half-gauged scarred systems are general and may lead to fruitful directions for higher dimension scarred physics and their robustness.

</details>


### [244] [Exchange self-energy and vertex corrections to static screening in the two-dimensional electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08309)
*Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文解析计算了二维电子气静态不可约极化率在环图之外的领头修正（两条交换自能图与交换顶点图，均为库仑耦合的一阶），并将其代入RPA几何级数得到静态介电函数与屏蔽相互作用。自能与顶点贡献各自发散但和有限，正好重现Hartree-Fock可压缩性；交换效应把2k_F处的单侧Kohn拐点变为尖锐的双侧峰，使Friedel振荡比RPA大2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPA屏蔽忽略了交换效应，在2k_F处仅给出单侧Kohn拐点，对二维电子气的静态极化率和屏蔽描述不够准确，需要引入超出环图的交换修正以改进这一描述并澄清其对Friedel振荡与配对的影响。

Method: 解析计算二维电子气静态不可约极化率的领头超出环图修正，即两条一阶库仑耦合的交换自能图与交换顶点图；将结果极化率代入RPA几何级数，得到静态介电函数和屏蔽相互作用；分别以闭式给出自能与顶点的发散部分并求和。

Result: 自能和顶点贡献各自发散，其和有限且精确重现Hartree-Fock可压缩性，从而重整化Thomas-Fermi波矢；交换把2k_F处单侧Kohn拐点变为尖锐双侧峰，是相对Thomas-Fermi和RPA屏蔽的定性改进；实空间中带电杂质周围Friedel振荡幅度比RPA大2-3倍，且在所研究范围内随距离增大。

Conclusion: 交换修正对二维电子气屏蔽具有定性重要意义，能正确给出双侧Kohn峰和增强的Friedel振荡，并讨论了对Kohn-Luttinger配对的含义，同时与关于相同图的早期工作进行了比较。

Abstract: We compute analytically the leading beyond-ring corrections to the static irreducible polarizability of the two-dimensional (2D) electron gas, namely the two exchange self-energy diagrams and the exchange vertex diagram, each of first order in the Coulomb coupling, and insert the resulting polarizability into the RPA geometric series to obtain the static dielectric function and screened interaction. The self-energy and vertex contributions individually diverge; we obtain both divergent pieces in closed form. Their sum is finite and reproduces exactly the Hartree-Fock compressibility, which renormalizes the Thomas-Fermi wave vector. We show that exchange converts the one-sided 2D Kohn kink at 2k_F into a sharp two-sided peak, a qualitative improvement over Thomas-Fermi and RPA screening. In real space this makes the Friedel oscillations around a charged impurity 2-3 times larger than in RPA, with an amplitude that increases with distance over the range studied; implications for Kohn-Luttinger pairing are discussed. Relation to earlier works on the same diagrams is discussed.

</details>


### [245] [Incoherent Spectral Weight Emerging from a Van Hove Singularity in a Kagome Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08356)
*C. -y. Lim,J. Deng,A. Korshunov,A. Kar,D. Subires,H. Li,Y. Jiang,H. Hu,E. Modin,P. Törmä,A. Kumar-Sharma,C. Shekhar,A. Louat,T. K. Kim,C. Felser,B. Andrei Bernevig,S. Blanco-Canosa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在LuFe6Ge6中发现垂直瀑布状谱特征,但其来源并非强电子关联,而可能与范霍夫奇点附近的非弹性散射有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARPES中观测到的瀑布状谱特征通常被归因于强电子关联或电子-玻色子耦合,但这一关联是否普遍成立尚需在结构简单的体系中检验。

Method: 对FeGe衍生家族中结构简单的kagome金属LuFe6Ge6,采用偏振依赖ARPES,并结合密度泛函理论与动力学平均场理论(DMFT)进行研究。

Result: 在L点观察到延伸数百meV结合能的显著垂直谱特征,其轨道特征主要为Fe dyz,能量起始位置与约位于EF以下0.3 eV的范霍夫奇点吻合。DMFT显示电子关联仅较弱,计算电子结构与实验体态能带高度一致。

Conclusion: 瀑布状特征可在无强电子关联的情况下出现,其成因可能来自出射光电子的非弹性散射,提示在解释此类特征时需考虑能带结构与光电子能量损失过程的作用。

Abstract: Waterfall-like spectral features observed by angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) are commonly associated with strong electronic correlations or electron-boson coupling. Here, we investigate this connection in the kagome metal LuFe6Ge6, a structurally simple member of the FeGe-derived family, using polarization-dependent ARPES, density functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory. We observe a pronounced vertical spectral feature at the L point extending over several hundred meV in binding energy. Its orbital character is identified as predominantly Fe dyz, and its energy onset coincides with a van Hove singularity located approximately 0.3 eV below EF . Despite the waterfall-like spectral response, dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) reveals only weak electronic correlations, while the calculated electronic structure closely reproduces the experimental bulk bands. We propose that the anomalous spectral weight may arise from inelastic scattering of the outgoing photoelectrons, potentially associated with the large density of states near the van Hove singularity. Our results suggest that waterfall-like features can emerge without strong electronic correlations and highlight the possible role of the underlying band structure and photoelectron energy-loss processes in their interpretation.

</details>


### [246] [Dynamical vertex approximation for retarded interactions: Application to the Hubbard-Holstein model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08512)
*Emin Moghadas,Samuele Giuli,Massimo Capone,Alessandro Toschi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将动力学顶点近似（DΓA）推广至含延迟电子相互作用的体系，提出"动力学-U DΓA"方法，并在Hubbard-Holstein模型上验证其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DΓA（阶梯版本）仅处理瞬时电子相互作用，无法描述电子与玻色子耦合导致的延迟（频率依赖）相互作用，需将其推广以兼顾非局域空间关联与延迟效应。

Method: 将最常用的阶梯版DΓA扩展至含延迟电子相互作用体系，命名为"dynamical-U DΓA"；在DΓA的局域关联基础上引入全尺度非局域空间关联；以耦合单一无色散声子模为例，在正方与立方晶格上求解Hubbard-Holstein模型，关注Hubbard相互作用较强、强电子关联与声子介导相互作用竞争的区间。

Result: 在三维模型的反铁磁相与二维的d波超导相两个算例中，分析了电子-声子耦合（含其频率依赖）对这两种不稳定性的影响。

Conclusion: "dynamical-U DΓA"可自洽地同时处理延迟相互作用与非局域空间关联，为研究强关联电子-玻色子耦合体系提供了可行且有效的理论框架。

Abstract: We extend the dynamical vertex approximation (D$Γ$A), in its most commonly used ladder-version, to systems with retarded electronic interactions. Such an extended scheme, which we name "dynamical-$U$ D$Γ$A", allows us to study strongly interacting electrons coupled to bosons including non-local spatial correlations on all length scales on top of the purely local ones captured by dynamical mean-field theory. We demonstrate the applicability of the method in the specific case of the coupling to a single dispersionless phononic mode, by studying the Hubbard-Holstein model on the square and cubic lattices, focusing on regimes of sizable Hubbard interactions where strong electronic correlations compete with phonon-mediated interactions. As illustrative examples, we consider the antiferromagnetic phase in the three-dimensional model and the d-wave superconductor in two dimensions, analyzing the effect of the electron-phonon coupling, including its frequency dependence, on both instabilities.

</details>


### [247] [Ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson approach to response functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08575)
*Lee Tsung-Han,Melnick Corey,Lanatà Nicola,Kotliar Gabriel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出gRISB理论下的有限温高斯涨落响应函数形式，应用于二维Hubbard模型，磁相边界与静态磁/电荷磁化率优于标准RISB和RPA，且动态磁化率在实频轴上直接计算，无需Matsubara截断或数值解析延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强关联电子体系中，响应函数（磁化率、电荷磁化率等）是理解自旋涨落、集体激发和相变的关键，但标准RISB与RPA对响应的描述不足，DMFT虽准确却计算代价高且动态响应常依赖Matsubara截断与数值解析延拓。作者希望发展一种有限温、在实频轴上直接可算、能接近DMFT精度的响应函数方法。

Method: 在ghost旋转不变从玻色子（gRISB）理论框架内建立有限温高斯涨落形式，用于计算响应函数；应用于二维Hubbard模型，计算磁相边界及静态自旋与电荷磁化率，并与DMFT、标准RISB和RPA对比；在RISB极限下恢复Kotliar-Ruckenstein响应；动态磁化率直接在实频轴上评估；另引入一种守恒准粒子近似，用缀饰格林函数和顶点修正表示响应。

Result: gRISB的磁相边界及静态自旋和电荷磁化率能够再现DMFT的趋势，显著优于标准RISB和RPA；在RISB层面可恢复Kotliar-Ruckenstein响应；动态磁化率分辨出顺磁振子、低能声模和高能Hubbard激发；由于直接在实频轴上计算，避免了Matsubara截断、随机采样和数值解析延拓。

Conclusion: 该工作为gRISB提供了一个有限温高斯涨落响应函数框架，能够以较低计算代价获得接近DMFT的磁响应与集体激发信息，并避免解析延拓相关问题，为强关联体系的响应与激发谱研究提供了实用途径。

Abstract: We propose a finite-temperature Gaussian-fluctuation formalism for response functions within ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson (gRISB) theory and apply it to the two-dimensional Hubbard model. The gRISB magnetic phase boundary and static spin and charge susceptibilities capture the trends of dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), significantly improving upon standard RISB and the random phase approximation (RPA). At the RISB level, our formalism recovers the Kotliar-Ruckenstein response. The dynamical susceptibilities resolve paramagnons, low-energy sound modes, and high-energy Hubbard excitations. Because they are evaluated directly on the real-frequency axis, the results avoid Matsubara truncation, stochastic sampling, and numerical analytic continuation. We also introduce a conserving quasiparticle approximation that expresses the response in terms of dressed Green's functions and vertex corrections.

</details>


### [248] [Chiral Central Charge from Real-Space Twist Operator Correlator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08788)
*Yunlong Zang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于扭曲算符关联函数的普适实空间公式，用于从实空间纠缠结构中直接提取二维有能隙量子系统的手性中心荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于纠缠的量子物相刻画近年取得重要进展，实空间公式因兼具理论与实验意义而备受关注。对于二维有能隙量子系统，现有方法缺乏直接从格点实空间纠缠结构提取手性中心荷的普适框架，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造N拷贝态 |ψ^(N)⟩=|ψ⟩^{⊗N}，定义扭曲算符 T_R(g)（按置换 g∈S_N 重排区域 R 内的 N 个复本），并研究关联函数 Z_{g,h}=⟨ψ^(N)|T_A(g)T_B(h)T_C(g)T_C(h)|ψ^(N)⟩。对满足球面条件的置换对 (g,h)，在合适的假设下于场论框架内推导该关联函数相位与手性中心荷的关系。

Result: 证明 Z_{g,h}/|Z_{g,h}| = (θ_g θ_h / θ_{gh})^{c_-}，即关联函数相位由手性中心荷 c_- 决定，其中 θ_g^{c_-} 是标记为 g∈S_N 的扭曲缺陷的拓扑自旋。该构造同时适用于玻色子和费米子系统，并在微观格点模型中进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: 研究建立了实空间纠缠结构与手性中心荷之间的普适联系，为直接在格点上刻画手性拓扑物相提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed substantial progress in the entanglement-based characterization of quantum phases of matter, with growing interest in real-space formulas due to their theoretical and experimental relevance. For two-dimensional gapped quantum systems, we propose a universal real-space formula for the chiral central charge based on the twist-operator correlator $Z_{g,h}=\langleψ^{(N)}\rvert\mathcal{T}_A(g)\mathcal{T}_B(h)\mathcal{T}_C(g)\mathcal{T}_C(h)\lvertψ^{(N)}\rangle$, where $\lvertψ^{(N)}\rangle=\lvertψ\rangle^{\otimes N}$ is the $N$-copy state and $g,h\in S_N$ are permutations. The twist operator $\mathcal{T}_R(g)$ permutes the $N$ replicas within region $R$ according to $g$. For pairs $(g,h)$ satisfying the spherical condition, we show that the phase of the correlator is determined by the chiral central charge $\mathfrak{c}_{-}$ through $Z_{g,h}/\left\lvert Z_{g,h}\right\rvert=(θ_gθ_h/θ_{gh})^{\mathfrak{c}_{-}}$, where $θ_g^{\mathfrak{c}_{-}}$ is the topological spin of the twist defect labeled by $g\in S_N$. The construction applies to both bosonic and fermionic systems. We derive the formula within a field-theoretic framework under suitable assumptions and further test it numerically in microscopic lattice models. Our results establish a universal connection between real-space entanglement structures and the chiral central charge, providing a systematic framework for characterizing chiral topological phases directly on the lattice.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [249] [Inverse construction of exact atmospheric models and the 3.7--4.4\,mHz acoustic resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07073)
*O. V. Kaptsov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将大气声重力波的传播建模为具有势函数Q的Klein-Gordon方程，通过构造正零能解对应的显式大气剖面，证明反射less剖面可推广；用Darboux-Crum势重构11–100 km标准大气并揭示4.4 mHz阻尼泛音缺失，进而提出有限厚度截止屏障；用Rosen-Morse势给出超几何共振条件，以3.76和4.37 mHz及泄漏品质因子173和20解释两振荡的差异阻尼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型爆炸喷发和地震会激发约3.7和4.4 mHz的近地大气振荡并与固体地球耦合，但为何这两个频率的振荡具有不同的阻尼特性尚缺乏解析解释；现有模型多局限于常势反射less剖面，无法刻画真实大气中截止屏障与波泄漏的作用，因此需要建立可解析、可对应实际大气结构且能解释观测谱的传播理论。

Method: 在静力理想气体大气中处理垂直传播的线性声重力波，用travel-time表述导出含势Q(τ)的Klein-Gordon方程。第一，对任意存在正零能解的势，通过求积显式构造对应的大气剖面，推广常Q反射less剖面；第二，采用Darboux-Crum势构造光滑大气，使11–100 km内与U.S. Standard Atmosphere温差在3 K内，且对截止频率以上的垂直波完全透明；第三，采用Rosen-Morse势得到闭式超几何共振条件，计算共振频率与泄漏品质因子，并对六个事件做全波计算验证。

Result: Darboux-Crum势大气在11–100 km内误差≤3 K，截止以上对垂直波完全透明，在4.4 mHz附近无阻尼泛音，截止以下的基模完全被陷；说明观测谱需要连接低层大气与热层的有限厚度截止屏障。Rosen-Morse势给出闭式超几何共振条件，共振频率3.76和4.37 mHz，泄漏品质因子分别为173和20；基模位于中间层截止屏障顶部以下、通过隧穿泄漏，而泛音位于屏障顶部以上，从而解释两振荡阻尼差异。全波计算在七个事件中的六个复现了3.7–3.8 mHz基模振荡。

Conclusion: 约3.7和4.4 mHz大气振荡的不同阻尼可由有限厚度截止屏障及基模隧穿泄漏、泛音位于屏障上方来解释；所建立的正零能解求积构造、Darboux-Crum大气和Rosen-Morse共振框架将反射less剖面推广到更一般势，并与观测谱及事件全波计算结果一致。

Abstract: Large explosive eruptions and earthquakes excite atmospheric oscillations near 3.7 and 4.4\,mHz that couple to the solid Earth. For vertically propagating linear acoustic--gravity waves in a hydrostatic ideal-gas atmosphere, the travel-time formulation gives a Klein--Gordon equation with a potential $Q(τ)$. We establish three main results. First, for every potential admitting a positive zero-energy solution, the corresponding atmospheric profiles are constructed explicitly by quadrature; this extends the known reflectionless profiles of constant $Q$. Second, Darboux--Crum potentials give a smooth atmosphere that reproduces the U.S. Standard Atmosphere between 11 and 100\,km to within 3\,K and is exactly transparent to vertical waves above its cut-off; it supports no damped overtone near 4.4\,mHz, and its ground-confined mode below the cut-off is perfectly trapped. The observed spectrum therefore requires a cut-off barrier of finite thickness connecting the lower atmosphere to the thermosphere. Third, a Rosen--Morse barrier gives an exactly solvable atmosphere with a closed-form hypergeometric resonance condition. Its resonances at 3.76 and 4.37\,mHz, with leakage quality factors 173 and 20, explain the different damping of the two oscillations: the fundamental lies below the top of the mesospheric cut-off barrier and leaks by tunnelling, whereas the overtone lies above it. Full-wave computations for event-specific atmospheres reproduce the reported 3.7--3.8\,mHz fundamental oscillation in all six events, of seven considered, in which such a mode was identified.

</details>


### [250] [Araki Cohomology of the de Sitter Scalar Discrete Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07090)
*Maryam Bajalan,Jean-Pierre Gazeau,Hamed Pejhan,Petr Siegl*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在dS协变不可分解Krein表示（Gupta-Bleuler三重态）基础上揭示其Araki上同调结构，并构造出四层精细化的Gupta-Bleuler四重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将SO_0(1,4)标量离散系列UIR的Krein实现与Araki标准Gupta-Bleuler三重态建立联系，并刻画其非分裂不可分解结构的上同调本质。

Method: 利用Krein-Gupta-Bleuler构造给出Araki标准三重态的具体实现，以其非平凡一阶上同调类（通过伴随关系相联系）编码非分裂结构；进一步对可约中间扇区分解为两个不可约分量。

Result: 证明中间扇区的分解诱导出扩张类的相应分解，得到Araki标准三重态的四层精细化即Gupta-Bleuler四重态，从而更精细地上同调分辨物理表示与其复共轭扇区。

Conclusion: Krein-Gupta-Bleuler构造是Araki标准Gupta-Bleuler三重态的具体实现，其四重态推广提供了区分物理表示与复共轭扇区的更精细上同调分辨。

Abstract: Building on the recently established de Sitter (dS)-covariant indecomposable Krein realization of the scalar discrete-series unitary irreducible representations $Π_{p,0}$, $p=1,2,\cdots$, of $\mathrm{SO}_0(1,4)$, naturally organized into a Gupta-Bleuler triplet, we uncover its underlying Araki-cohomological structure. We show that the Krein-Gupta-Bleuler construction provides a concrete realization of Araki's standard Gupta-Bleuler triplet, with its non-split indecomposable structure encoded by non-trivial first cohomology classes related by adjunction. We further show that the reducible intermediate sector naturally resolves into its two irreducible components, inducing a corresponding decomposition of the extension classes and yielding a four-layer refinement of Araki's standard triplet. The resulting Gupta-Bleuler quadruplet provides a finer cohomological resolution, distinguishing the physical representation from its complex-conjugate sector.

</details>


### [251] [Conformal invariance of the six-vertex model's height function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07454)
*Emile Averous,Hugo Duminil-Copin,Tiancheng He,Piet Lammers,Ioan Manolescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在分段常数边界条件下，单连通区域中六顶点模型的高度函数收敛于Dirichlet边界条件的GFF，从而得到模型缩放极限的共形不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立六顶点模型在连续谱参数范围内缩放极限的共形不变性，这是此前研究未能覆盖的。

Method: 论证依赖全平面GFF收敛，结合反射正性（Reflexion Positivity）与定性估计。

Result: 证明适用于广泛的谱参数Δ范围（包括Δ接近0以及介于-1与-1/2之间），成为首个对连续模型族给出共形不变性结果的工作。

Conclusion: 该结论对q=4的FK渗流和Ashkin-Teller模型等其他模型亦有推论，作者将在未来工作中进一步研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the height function of the six-vertex model in simply connected domains with piecewise constant boundary conditions converges to the Gaussian Free Field (GFF) with Dirichlet boundary conditions. This implies the conformal invariance of the scaling limit of the model.
  The argument relies on the full plane GFF convergence combined with Reflexion Positivity and qualitative estimates. The proof works for a wide range of spectral parameters $Δ$ (among which $Δ$ close to $0$, and between $-1$ and $-1/2$) making this paper the first instance of a conformal invariance result for a continuum of models. The paper has consequences for other models, including the FK percolation with cluster-weight $q=4$ and the Ashkin-Teller model, which we will pursue in future work.

</details>


### [252] [Spontaneous symmetry breaking at any temperature in a local one-dimensional model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07693)
*Andrew Lucas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 在一维无限格点上构造具螺旋对称群 ℤ²⋊ℤ 的经典统计物理模型，证明任意有限温度下存在不可数无穷多个 DLR（Gibbs）态，表现为自发对称破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在寻找一个能在一维、任意温度下展现自发对称破缺的例子，其关键在于螺旋对称群将内部（flavor）对称性与空间平移耦合，且为非紧、不与平移对易，从而绕开 Mermin-Wagner 定理的限制。

Method: 在一维无限格点上构造最近邻相互作用的经典模型，局部状态空间为 ℤ²（另有局部状态空间为 ℝ² 的变体）；利用 "helical" 对称群 ℤ²⋊ℤ；对任意 0<β<∞ 构造被 ℝ² 参数化、具自由 ℤ² 作用的一族 DLR 态；并将问题正则化到任意大但有限的周期边界系统，分析 Gibbs 态的测度集中。

Result: 对任意 0<β<∞，构造出不可数无穷多个 DLR 态，参数空间为 ℝ²，具自由 ℤ² 对称群作用，可解释为任意温度下的自发对称破缺；最一般的 DLR 态对应分离两个渐近"对称扇区"的无限局域畴壁序列；虽任何可归一化 Gibbs 态必然破坏可数无穷的自由作用对称群，但有限周期系统（唯一 Gibbs 态）中测度集中在互不相交且相距很远的对称相关簇中，支持无限直线上的自发对称破缺解释。

Conclusion: 该模型在任意温度下表现出自发对称破缺，并可与 Mermin-Wagner 定理相容——因为其连续螺旋对称是非紧的且不与平移对易。局部状态空间为 ℝ² 的修改模型亦必须在任意温度下破坏该连续螺旋对称。

Abstract: We present a one-dimensional classical statistical physics model on an infinite lattice with nearest-neighbor interactions and local state space $\mathbb{Z}^2$, with a ``helical" symmetry group $\mathbb{Z}^2\rtimes \mathbb{Z}$ mixing flavor symmetry with spatial translation. For any $0<β<\infty$, we construct an uncountable infinity of DLR (Gibbs) states parameterized by $\mathbb{R}^2$, with a free $\mathbb{Z}^2$ flavor symmetry group action; as such, we interpret the model as exhibiting spontaneous symmetry breaking at any temperature. The most general DLR state we construct is interpreted as an infinite sequence of localized domain walls separating a pair of asymptotic ``symmetry sectors". Although any normalizable Gibbs state necessarily breaks a countably infinite freely-acting symmetry group, a natural regularization of the problem to (arbitrarily) large but finite systems with periodic boundary conditions has measure concentration in disjoint and far-separated symmetry-related clusters in the (now unique) Gibbs state, which justifies our interpretation of spontaneous symmetry breaking on the infinite line. A modification of the model with local state space $\mathbb{R}^2$ must break a continuous helical symmetry at any temperature. This is not a contradiction with the Mermin-Wagner Theorem, because this continuous helical symmetry is non-compact and does not commute with translation.

</details>


### [253] [A mathematical analogy between certain hollow vortex and bubble flows - existence, uniqueness, and identical boundary shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07709)
*Valentin D. Kunz,Saleh Tanveer,Darren G. Crowdy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维无粘流体中两个物理上不同的定常自由边界问题（剪切流中的空心涡与带表面张力的常压气泡），基于Crowdy-Tanveer的涡-气泡对应关系，证明二者共享同一数学表述。分别用序列空间不动点法（涡）与非线性奇异积分算子不动点法（气泡）建立局部存在性、唯一性与解析性，并给出定义参数空间中实解析曲面的标量可解性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡与气泡虽在物理上不同（无表面张力vs有表面张力、常涡量vs无旋、外部流动不同），却存在Crowdy-Tanveer建立的数学对应。作者希望把这一对应从形式层面提升为严格的解析理论，统一处理两类自由边界问题，并厘清何种参数关系能使二者具有相同自由边界形状。

Method: 涡问题：将Crowdy-Tanveer的序列空间不动点方法推广至简单剪切、三阶及线性应变无穷远条件情形。气泡问题：发展独立的（相对于涡问题的）存在性理论，将其归约为非线性奇异积分算子不动点问题，并推导出一个标量可解性条件。

Result: 涡问题获得新的局部存在性、唯一性与解析性结果；气泡问题在标量条件定义的实解析曲面上得到局部唯一解，边界实解析且速度场全纯可延拓；并证明该标量可解性条件等价于一泛函共形映射恒等式。仅需该标量参数条件，涡与气泡即可拥有相同自由边界形状。

Conclusion: 标量可解性条件（等价于泛函共形映射恒等式）刻画了参数空间中一个实解析曲面，其上涡与气泡问题共享同一自由边界形状，尽管其外部流动不同，从而在严格解析框架下实现了两类问题的统一。

Abstract: We study two physically distinct steady free-boundary problems in a two-dimensional inviscid fluid: a hollow vortex in a constant-vorticity flow subject to simple shear, third order and linear strain at infinity, without surface tension, and an irrotational flow past a constant-pressure bubble subject to surface tension, linear strain, and circulation at infinity. Building on the mathematical vortex-bubble correspondence recently developed by Crowdy and Tanveer, we show that both problems are linked by a common mathematical formulation. For the vortex problem, we extend the sequence-space fixed-point approach of Crowdy and Tanveer to obtain new local existence, uniqueness, and analyticity results. For the bubble problem, we develop a separate existence theory, reducing it to a nonlinear singular integral operator fixed-point problem and deriving a scalar solvability condition that defines a realanalytic surface in the physical parameter space. On this surface we obtain locally unique bubble solutions with real-analytic boundaries and holomorphically extendible velocity fields. Finally, we prove that the scalar solvability condition is equivalent to a functional conformal mapping identity. The scalar condition between the parameters is enough for the vortex and bubble problems to have the same free-boundary shape, despite their different exterior flows.

</details>


### [254] [Pulsation of quantum walk on finite graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07768)
*Taisuke Hosaka,Etsuo Segawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究带权图上的离散时间量子游走，边割集上的边赋予小权重 ε。当 ε→0 且时间尺度 t=Θ(ε^{-1/2}) 时，连通分量之间的发现概率渐近由约化图上的连续时间波动方程描述，该方程由对称加权拉普拉斯算子支配，并与约化图上经典弹簧-质量系统的牛顿运动方程同形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究带权图中的"弱边"（边割集上的小权重 ε）如何控制图在极限 ε→0 时的分解行为，以及量子游走在中间时间尺度上的跨分量传输规律，从而揭示量子游走与经典波动/力学模型之间的联系。

Method: 对带权图上的离散时间量子游走进行渐近分析：将边割集上的边赋予小权重 ε>0，在 ε 充分小、时间尺度 t=Θ(ε^{-1/2}) 下推导连通分量间发现概率的极限形式，构造以连通分量为顶点、弱边为边的约化图，并建立其上的连续时间波动方程与对称加权拉普拉斯算子。

Result: 在 ε 充分小且 t=Θ(ε^{-1/2}) 时，连通分量之间的发现概率的主项由约化图上的连续时间波动方程渐近给出；该波动方程由约化图及分量内弧数决定的对称加权拉普拉斯算子支配；主导项发现概率与分量内部细节结构无关；该波动方程与约化图上经典弹簧-质量系统的牛顿运动方程具有相同形式。

Conclusion: 弱边驱动的量子游走在中间时间尺度上表现出普适的波动行为，其跨分量动力学仅依赖约化图的粗粒化结构，与分量内部结构无关，并与经典弹簧-质量系统的牛顿方程对应，建立了量子游走与经典波动/力学之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We study discrete-time quantum walks on weighted graphs, where every edge in an edge cut set is assigned a small weight $ε>0$. The parameter $ε$ represents the strength of the connections through the cut edges: as $ε\to 0$, these connections vanish, and the graph decomposes into the connected components obtained by removing the cut edges. We refer to these weighted cut edges as {\it weak edges}. We show that, for sufficiently small $ε$ and on the time scale $t=Θ(ε^{-1/2})$, the finding probabilities between the connected components are asymptotically described by a continuous-time wave equation on a reduced graph, whose vertices represent the connected components and whose edges represent the weak edges connecting them. The wave equation is governed by a symmetric weighted Laplacian determined by the reduced graph and the number of arcs contained in the components. Consequently, finding probabilities of leading-term are independent of the detailed internal structures of the components. We further show that this wave equation has the same form as Newton's equation of motion for a classical spring-mass system on the reduced graph.

</details>


### [255] [Convergence Without Finite Exactness in the Eigenstate Quantum Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07775)
*Ye Zhou,Daniel Keren*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明量子力学本征态 bootstrap 可在每个有限阶都不精确（存在持续间隙）却仍收敛到物理矩集，并通过准精确可解六次振子显式构造该现象，进而给出一般多项式 Schrödinger 算子的收敛性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征态量子力学 bootstrap 通常通过逐阶增加矩与约束逼近物理矩集，但有限阶截断是否必然精确、以及有限阶间隙是否会消失此前缺乏严格判据。作者希望厘清有限阶非精确性与渐近收敛之间的关系，并构造显式反例说明"有限阶永远不精确"并不妨碍收敛。

Method: 以一维准精确可解六次振子为模型，在已知本征值（含激发态）处考察中心可观测量 u² 的上下界；在保留矩空间中加入任意有限多项式 Gram 检验与全部本征态湮灭关系，甚至加入权重严格为正的有限局部化约束，检验是否出现有限阶 sum-of-squares 证书。随后对满足显式 sum-of-squares 束缚与导数界的一般多项式 Schrödinger 算子证明收敛性，利用本征态关系导出关于截断阶一致的矩估计，并构造正则 Schrödinger 表示。最后将固定可观测量障碍与可行位置矩（每阶无正表示测度）结合，给出四模扩展。

Result: 在六次振子模型中，每个有限阶上 u² 的下界严格低于、上界严格高于其物理值，即使在加入任意有限多项式 Gram 检验与可见的湮灭关系后依然如此，因此中心可观测量的任一侧都不存在模本征态湮灭算子的有限 sum-of-squares 证书，且该间隙在任意有限正权重局部化约束下持续存在。对一般多项式 Schrödinger 算子，一致的矩估计导出支撑于指定本征空间上的正则 Schrödinger 表示，从而证明收敛。四模构造在第三阶给出严格正定矩矩阵的非物理可行点。

Conclusion: 有限阶非精确性与渐近收敛可以并存：bootstrap 的有限阶间隙不构成收敛障碍，但可能导致有限阶可行集包含严格正定矩矩阵的非物理点，提示单纯依赖矩矩阵正定性与有限 sum-of-squares 检验不足以判定物理性。

Abstract: The eigenstate quantum-mechanical bootstrap can converge to the physical moment set while remaining nonexact at every finite order. We prove this for a one-dimensional quasi-exactly solvable sextic oscillator at explicitly known eigenvalues, including excited levels. At every finite order, the lower and upper bounds on $u^2$ remain strictly below and above its physical value, even after adjoining arbitrary finite polynomial Gram tests and all eigenstate-annihilator relations visible in the retained moment space. Hence neither side of the centered observable admits a finite sum-of-squares certificate modulo the eigenstate annihilator. The gap persists under any finite set of additional localizing constraints whose weights are strictly positive on polynomial vectors. We then prove convergence for polynomial Schrödinger operators satisfying explicit sum-of-squares confinement and derivative bounds. The eigenstate relations give moment estimates uniform in the truncation order; these yield a regular Schrödinger representation supported on the prescribed eigenspace. Finally, a four-mode extension combines this fixed-observable obstruction with feasible position moments having no positive representing measure at every finite order. At level three, this construction yields nonphysical feasible points with strictly positive definite moment matrices.

</details>


### [256] [Localization Dichotomy for Hall Insulators on the Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08278)
*Alex Bols*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了非周期霍尔绝缘体在晶格上的局域化二分猜想：对于 $l^2(\mathbb{Z}^2)\otimes\mathbb{C}^n$ 上带隙且指数局域的哈密顿量，其费米投影子 $P$ 存在指数局域化的 Wannier 基当且仅当 $P$ 的陈数消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非周期（无序或一般非周期）霍尔绝缘体的拓扑分类长期缺乏严格基础。周期性体系中已有著名的局域化二分结论（陈数非零则无法指数局域），但向非周期体系的推广一直是公开的猜想，本文旨在证明这一猜想。

Method: 研究对象为 $l^2(\mathbb{Z}^2)\otimes\mathbb{C}^n$ 上具有谱隙且指数局域的哈密顿量，考察其费米投影子 $P$ 的局域化性质。通过建立 $P$ 是否存在指数局域化 Wannier 基与陈数是否为零之间的等价关系来证明该二分命题。

Result: 证明了局域化二分猜想在非周期情形下成立：费米投影子 $P$ 存在指数局域化 Wannier 基，当且仅当其陈数消失。

Conclusion: 非周期霍尔绝缘体的体拓扑不变量（陈数）完全刻画了其 Wannier 基的指数局域化可能性，为非周期拓扑绝缘体的严格分类提供了关键基础。

Abstract: We prove the localization dichotomy conjecture for non-periodic Hall insulators on the lattice. That is, we show that the Fermi projector $P$ of a gapped exponentially local Hamiltonian on $l^2(\mathbb{Z}^2) \otimes \mathbb{C}^n$ admits an exponentially localized Wannier basis if and only if $P$ has vanishing Chern number.

</details>


### [257] [Stability of Matter Interacting with Classical Non-Abelian Gauge Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08313)
*Robin Reuvers,Jan Philip Solovej,David Stuart*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究色荷费米子与经典非阿贝尔规范场耦合时的物质稳定性，证明结果与电（阿贝尔）情况一致，关键在于建立了对矩阵值电荷一致成立、替换牛顿定理均匀带电球面的静电不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物质稳定性指N个带电量子粒子的基态能量下界为-CN（C与N无关）。此前该性质主要在阿贝尔（电）情形下被证明，而色荷费米子与经典非阿贝尔规范场耦合时，场是与物质同等的动力学变量且需对两者同时取能量极小，电荷为矩阵且每对粒子存在吸引色通道，原有阿贝尔证明的关键步骤失效，故需重新考察稳定性是否成立。

Method: 将经典非阿贝尔规范场作为与物质同等的动力学变量，对物质与场同时极小化能量。核心技术工具是一个对矩阵值电荷一致成立（对场一致）的静电不等式：用协变调和延拓的表面电荷替代牛顿定理中的均匀带电球面。结果对任意紧致规范群成立，且多数结论适用于有限能量的非光滑场。

Result: 非相对论性无自旋费米子对任意耦合常数α均稳定；相对论性无自旋费米子与非相对论自旋½费米子在α较小时稳定、α较大时不稳定；狄拉克费米子取决于正能子空间选择——采用完整狄拉克算子的正能子空间时小α稳定，采用自由正能子空间时对任意α均不稳定；两种玻色子不稳定。所有情形结果均与电（阿贝尔）情形相同。

Conclusion: 色荷费米子与经典非阿贝尔规范场耦合的物质稳定性定性上与电情形一致：无自旋非相对论费米子恒稳定，相对论无自旋及非相对论自旋½费米子存在α阈值，狄拉克费米子依赖正能子空间选取，双种玻色子不稳定；建立的矩阵值电荷静电不等式是关键，结论对任意紧致规范群成立。

Abstract: Stability of matter is the property that the ground state energy of $N$ charged quantum particles is bounded below by $-CN$, with $C$ independent of $N$. We ask whether this holds for colour-charged fermions interacting with a classical non-abelian gauge field. The field is a dynamical variable on the same footing as the matter, and the energy is minimized over both. The charges are matrices, and every pair of particles has an attractive colour channel. Non-relativistic spinless fermions are stable for every value of the coupling constant $α$. Relativistic spinless fermions and non-relativistic spin-$\frac12$ fermions are stable for small $α$ and unstable for large $α$. For Dirac fermions the choice of positive-energy subspace decides: with that of the full Dirac operator they are stable for small $α$, with the free one unstable for every $α$. Two species of bosons are unstable. In every case the outcome is the same as for electric charges, although essential steps of the abelian proofs fail. The key tool is an electrostatic inequality for matrix-valued charges, uniform in the field, which replaces the uniformly charged sphere of Newton's theorem by the surface charge of a covariant harmonic extension. The stability results hold for every compact gauge group, and most of them for non-smooth fields of finite energy.

</details>


### [258] [An unified approach for the (inverted) quantum harmonic oscillator and the Berry-Keating hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08437)
*Fabio Bagarello,Emanuele Balistreri,Sergey Kuzhel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出统一视角处理谐振子、倒谐振子与Berry-Keating三类二次哈密顿量，统一求解离散谱与（广义）本征态，并将其视为su(1,1)及非厄米推广的生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三类二次哈密顿量（谐振子、倒谐振子、Berry-Keating）在量子力学中广受关注，但此前多被分别处理；作者希望建立统一框架以同时刻画其谱结构、本征态及代数（李代数生成元）性质，并推广至非厄米情形。

Method: 构建统一视角：对三类二次哈密顿量采用统一方法确定其离散谱并推导（广义）本征态；进一步将它们解释为su(1,1)代数及其在非厄米生成元情形下的推广版本的生成元。

Result: 给出了三类二次哈密顿量离散谱的统一确定方法，得到了相应的（广义）本征态，并揭示了它们与su(1,1)代数及其非厄米推广之间的生成元关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一视角能够将谐振子、倒谐振子与Berry-Keating哈密顿量纳入同一代数与谱分析框架，并可自然延伸至非厄米生成元情形。

Abstract: We propose an unified view to three well known quadratic Hamiltonians widely considered in quantum mechanics, i.e. those of the harmonic oscillator, of the inverted harmonic oscillator, and the Berry-Keating Hamiltonian. We describe an unified approach to determine the discrete spectrum of these operators, and to deduce their (generalized) eigenstates. We also look at these Hamiltonians as the generators of $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$, and of a generalized version of it relevant in presence of non Hermitian generators.

</details>


### [259] [Conformal invariance of the planar four-state Potts model and its random-cluster representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08507)
*Emile Averous,Hugo Duminil-Copin,Tiancheng He,Piet Lammers,Ioan Manolescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明了 Z² 上 q=4 的临界随机簇模型环路在标度极限下收敛到 CLE(4)，并由此推出四态 Potts 模型的共形不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界统计物理模型的标度极限与共形不变性是概率论与统计物理的核心问题。此前对 q=4 的随机簇模型（等价于四态 Potts 模型）缺乏严格证明，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究 Z² 上簇权重 q=4 的临界随机簇模型，通过分析其环路（簇边界）在大尺度下的行为，证明环路分布收敛到 CLE(4)；随后借助 Edwards-Sokal 耦合，将随机簇模型的结果转移到 Potts 模型上。

Result: 证明了环路在标度极限下收敛到 CLE(4)，并据此推出四态 Potts 模型具有共形不变性。

Conclusion: 建立了 q=4 临界随机簇模型与 CLE(4) 之间的标度极限对应关系，确立了四态 Potts 模型的共形不变性，为非整数 q 及更一般模型的极限研究提供了范例。

Abstract: For the critical random-cluster model on $\mathbb Z^2$ with cluster-weight $q=4$, we prove the convergence of loops to CLE(4) in the scaling limit. Conformal invariance of the four-state Potts model is then deduced via the Edwards-Sokal coupling.

</details>


### [260] [Nematic order in monomer-dimer models of Heilmann and Lieb with lateral attraction in dimensions $d\ge 2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08600)
*Qidong He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究Heilmann--Lieb液晶模型（II和V）在任意维数$d\ge2$的推广，在化学势$\mu$、硬核排斥与平行二聚体侧向吸引（耦合$b>0$）下，证明了在附加条件$(4d-6)b>\mu$时存在向列序，并给出平移无序、Gibbs测度唯一性及取向相内各向异性指数衰减关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heilmann--Lieb于1979年提出的二维Model II与三维Model V在高维推广下的液晶序此前仅有猜想：当$\mu+2(d-1)b>0$时低温应出现液晶序，但缺乏严格证明。本文旨在对这一猜想在更广泛维数$d\ge2$上给出严格结果，并刻画相内的平移序与关联性质。

Method: 研究超立方格点上二聚体模型，二聚体间存在硬核排斥与平行二聚体间的侧向吸引（耦合$b>0$），化学势为$\mu$。取向序的处理沿用作者近期关于Model I的方法；平移序缺失的证明采用Sheffield(2005)意义下自行实现的团簇交换（cluster swapping）论证。附加物理条件$(4d-6)b>\mu$对应失配二聚体在一维参考系统关联长度尺度上稀少的物理机制。

Result: 对所有$d\ge2$，在$\mu+2(d-1)b>0$及附加条件$(4d-6)b>\mu$下证明了向列序（nematic order）的存在，同时证明了平移序缺失，并由此推出Gibbs测度唯一性以及每个取向相内关联的各向异性指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该工作将Heilmann--Lieb液晶模型的液晶序猜想从$d=2,3$推广并严格化到所有$d\ge2$，补充了平移无序性、Gibbs测度唯一性以及在取向相中的各向异性指数衰减关联，为高维液晶模型的严格统计力学刻画提供了新结果。

Abstract: We study the $d$-dimensional generalization of Models II ($d=2$) and V ($d=3$) for liquid crystals introduced by Heilmann--Lieb (1979), which consists of dimers on the hypercubic lattice at chemical potential $μ$, interacting via a hard-core repulsion and a lateral attraction between parallel dimers with coupling constant $b>0$. For $d\in\{2,3\}$, these models were conjectured to exhibit liquid-crystalline order at low temperatures provided that $μ+2(d-1)b>0$. We establish nematic order for all $d\ge 2$ under the additional condition $(4d-6)b>μ$, which corresponds to the physical regime in which misaligned dimers are rare on scales comparable to the correlation length of the one-dimensional reference system. While the treatment of orientational order here follows the approach of our recent work on Model I, the proof of the absence of translational order relies on a custom implementation of cluster swapping in the sense of Sheffield (2005). Consequently, we deduce uniqueness of Gibbs measure and anisotropic exponential decay of correlations within each oriented phase.

</details>
